| accepted_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 加工师接单时刻（抢单或点「接单」） |
| completed_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 完成时刻 |
| reassigned_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 改派时刻 |
| reassign_from_operator_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 改派前加工师 ID |
| reassign_reason | VARCHAR(256) | Y | NULL | 改派原因（改派必填） |
| **审计组** | | | | |
| assigned_by | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 派工人 ID（自动派工为 0） |
| assigned_by_name | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 派工人姓名（快照） |
| remark | VARCHAR(512) | Y | NULL | 备注 |
| created_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 创建时间 |
| updated_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 更新时间（触发器维护） |
| is_deleted | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 逻辑删除 |

**索引设计：**

| 索引名 | 类型 | 字段 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `pk_m10_lab_assign` | 主键 | `id` | — |
| `uk_m10_lab_assign_no` | 唯一 | `assign_no` | 派工号唯一 |
| `uk_m10_lab_assign_active_step` | 唯一（部分） | `step_id` WHERE `status = 'ACTIVE' AND step_id IS NOT NULL` | **同一工序同时只能有一个有效派工** |
| `uk_m10_lab_assign_active_job` | 唯一（部分） | `lab_job_id` WHERE `status = 'ACTIVE' AND step_id IS NULL` | 整单派工同时只有一条有效 |
| `idx_m10_lab_assign_operator` | B-Tree（部分） | `operator_id`, `assigned_at` WHERE `status = 'ACTIVE'` | **加工师当前负载（派工算法输入）** |
| `idx_m10_lab_assign_job` | B-Tree | `lab_job_id`, `assigned_at` DESC | 加工单派工历史 |
| `idx_m10_lab_assign_operator_day` | B-Tree | `operator_id`, `assigned_at` | 加工师日派工量统计（P09） |
| `idx_m10_lab_assign_status` | B-Tree | `status`, `store_id` | 改派与取消筛选 |

**约束说明：**

```sql
-- 派工号格式：PG + 8 位日期 + 5 位序号
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_assign
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_assign_no_format
    CHECK (assign_no ~ '^PG[0-9]{13}$');

-- 派工方式枚举
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_assign
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_assign_type
    CHECK (assign_type IN ('MANUAL', 'AUTO', 'GRAB', 'REASSIGN', 'SYSTEM'));

-- 派工状态枚举
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_assign
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_assign_status
    CHECK (status IN ('ACTIVE', 'COMPLETED', 'REASSIGNED', 'CANCELLED'));

-- 派工原因枚举
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_assign
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_assign_reason
    CHECK (assign_reason IS NULL
           OR assign_reason IN ('URGENT', 'OVERDUE', 'SKILL_MATCH', 'LOAD_BALANCE',
                                'OUTSOURCE_RETURN', 'MANUAL_OVERRIDE'));

-- 优先级与负载非负
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_assign
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_assign_numeric
    CHECK (priority_at_assign BETWEEN 1 AND 9
           AND operator_pending_count >= 0
           AND operator_load_minutes >= 0
           AND (operator_capacity_minutes IS NULL OR operator_capacity_minutes > 0));

-- 按工序派工必须有 step_id 与 step_seq；整单派工两者都为 NULL
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_assign
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_assign_step_pair
    CHECK ((step_id IS NULL AND step_seq IS NULL) OR (step_id IS NOT NULL AND step_seq IS NOT NULL));

-- 改派必须有原因、改派前加工师、改派时刻
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_assign
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_assign_reassign
    CHECK (status <> 'REASSIGNED'
           OR (reassign_reason IS NOT NULL AND reassigned_at IS NOT NULL
               AND reassign_from_operator_id IS NOT NULL
               AND reassign_from_operator_id <> operator_id));

-- 已完成必须有完成时刻，且不早于派工时刻
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_assign
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_assign_completed
    CHECK (status <> 'COMPLETED'
           OR (completed_at IS NOT NULL AND completed_at >= assigned_at));

-- 接单时刻不早于派工时刻
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_assign
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_assign_accepted
    CHECK (accepted_at IS NULL OR accepted_at >= assigned_at);

COMMENT ON TABLE m10_lab_assign IS '加工派工表：记录每道工序派给谁、何时派、改派历史；status=ACTIVE 的记录数即加工师实时负载';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m10_lab_assign.queue_position IS '派工时的队列位次，用于复盘"加急单是否真的插到了队首"';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m10_lab_assign.operator_load_minutes IS '派工时该加工师已排入的标准工时合计，与 capacity 比较判断是否超载';
```

**派工排序算法（写入 `queue_position` 的依据）：**

```
【排序键：四段式，字典序比较】

  ORDER BY
    ① CASE WHEN is_urgent THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,        -- 加急永远第一
    ② CASE WHEN is_overdue THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,       -- 逾期第二
    ③ priority ASC,                                  -- 优先级数值小优先
    ④ promised_at ASC,                               -- 承诺时刻早的先做
    ⑤ created_at ASC                                 -- 同时刻按建单先后

【为什么"加急"排在"逾期"之前】
  逾期单已经违约，再做也不可能挽回违约事实；而加急单（顾客付了加急费
  或医生要求当日取镜）还有机会按期交付。M10-06 的取镜满意度统计显示，
  逾期单最终交付时间的边际收益远低于加急单按期率。若医院希望逾期优先，
  通过系统参数 lab.assign.overdue_first=true 可切换。

【产能估算与可派判断】

  加工师当日可用产能（分钟）=
      班次剩余时长（分钟）
    × 加工师效率系数（熟练工 1.0 / 中级 0.85 / 学徒 0.65）
    × 多任务并行系数（磨边机可同时磨 2 片 → 1.2；手工装框 → 1.0）

  剩余可承接产能（分钟）=
      当日可用产能 − Σ(该加工师 ACTIVE 派工的工序 standard_minutes)
      − Σ(该加工师当日已完成工序 actual_minutes)

  可派条件：剩余可承接产能 ≥ 本工序 standard_minutes × 1.2（20% 缓冲）

【预计完工时刻（对客承诺）算法】

  普通单（无外协）：
    planned_finish_at = 派工时刻
                      + Σ(本单剩余工序 standard_minutes × 1.3)   ← 30% 缓冲
                      + 排队等待时长（= 前面加工师手上单子的剩余工时）

  含外协工序：
    planned_finish_at = 派工时刻
                      + Σ(外协前工序标准工时 × 1.3)
                      + 外协供应商标准回片天数 × 24 小时          ← 见 4.11
                      + Σ(外协后工序标准工时 × 1.3)

  定制片（厂家来片）：
    expected_piece_at = 付款时刻 + lead_time_days × 24 小时       ← M01-08
    planned_start_at  = expected_piece_at + 2 小时（到片验收）
    planned_finish_at = planned_start_at + Σ(全部工序标准工时 × 1.3)

  对客承诺 promised_at = max(planned_finish_at, 建单时刻 + 30 分钟)

【自动排程的触发点】
  ① P-17 on-order-paid 建单后立即跑一次（为每道工序给出候选加工师）
  ② 每 5 分钟 job_m10_lab_autodispatch 扫描 PENDING 单并自动派工
  ③ 加工师完成一道工序后触发下一道候选计算
  ④ 改派/请假/外协回片时人工触发
```

---

### 4.11 表：`m10_lab_outsource`（外协加工单表）

**用途：** 记录外送加工的全过程：外送给谁、什么时候送出、承诺几天回、实际什么时候回、验收是否合格、费用多少。对应状态机中的 `OUTSOURCED` 分支。

| 字段名 | 类型 | 允许空 | 默认 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | BIGINT | N | IDENTITY | 主键 |
| outsource_no | VARCHAR(32) | N | — | 外协单号 `WX` + YYYYMMDD + 4 位 |
| lab_job_id | BIGINT | N | — | 加工单 ID |
| lab_job_no | VARCHAR(32) | N | — | 加工单号（冗余） |
| step_id | BIGINT | N | — | 外协工序 ID（`m10_lab_job_step.id`） |
| step_seq | SMALLINT | N | — | 外协工序序号（冗余） |
| process_code | VARCHAR(32) | N | — | 外协工序编码（M01-08 只读） |
| process_type | VARCHAR(16) | N | — | 外协工序类型（如 `COATING` 镀膜、`TINTING` 染色） |
| store_id | BIGINT | N | — | 门店 ID |
| **供应商组** | | | | |
| supplier_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 外协供应商 ID（M01-04 `mdm_supplier.id`，只读引用） |
| supplier_code | VARCHAR(32) | N | — | 供应商编码（来自 M01-08 工序属性 `outsourced_supplier`） |
| supplier_name | VARCHAR(200) | N | — | 供应商名称（快照） |
| supplier_contact | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 供应商联系人（快照） |
| supplier_phone | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 供应商联系电话（快照） |
| **外送与回片组** | | | | |
| qty | NUMERIC(12,4) | N | 1 | 外送数量（片数/副数） |
| sent_at | TIMESTAMP | N | — | 外送时刻 |
| expected_return_at | TIMESTAMP | N | — | **承诺回片时刻**（= 外送时刻 + 标准回片天数） |
| standard_return_days | SMALLINT | N | 3 | 标准回片天数（来自供应商系数或系统参数） |
| returned_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 实际回片时刻 |
| is_overdue | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 是否逾期（回片超 `expected_return_at`） |
| overdue_days | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 逾期天数（由定时任务刷新） |
| **验收组** | | | | |
| send_by | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 外送人 ID |
| send_by_name | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 外送人姓名（快照） |
| received_by | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 接收人 ID |
| received_by_name | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 接收人姓名（快照） |
| inspect_result | VARCHAR(16) | Y | NULL | 验收结果 `PASS`/`FAIL`/`REWORK`/`RETURN_TO_SUPPLIER` |
| inspect_remark | VARCHAR(512) | Y | NULL | 验收说明（不合格必填） |
| defect_type | VARCHAR(24) | Y | NULL | 外协件缺陷类型（`SCRATCH` 划伤 / `COATING_DEFECT` 膜层不良 / `POWER_ERROR` 度数错 / `OTHER`） |
| return_times | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 返修次数（外协退回重做次数） |
| **费用组** | | | | |
| outsource_fee | NUMERIC(10,2) | N | 0 | 外协加工费，单位元（成本项，非对客价格） |
| fee_settle_status | VARCHAR(16) | N | 'UNSETTLED' | 费用结算状态 `UNSETTLED`/`SETTLED`/`VOID` |
| settle_ref_no | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 结算参考号（M15 或财务凭证号） |
| **状态组** | | | | |
| status | VARCHAR(16) | N | 'SENT' | `DRAFT`/`SENT`/`RECEIVED`/`INSPECTED`/`REJECTED`/`CLOSED`/`CANCELLED` |
| cancel_reason | VARCHAR(256) | Y | NULL | 取消原因 |
| remark | VARCHAR(512) | Y | NULL | 备注 |
| version | INTEGER | N | 1 | 乐观锁版本号 |
| created_by | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 创建人 |
| created_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 创建时间 |
| updated_by | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 更新人 |
| updated_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 更新时间（触发器维护） |
| is_deleted | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 逻辑删除 |

**索引设计：**

| 索引名 | 类型 | 字段 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `pk_m10_lab_outsource` | 主键 | `id` | — |
| `uk_m10_lab_outsource_no` | 唯一（部分） | `outsource_no` WHERE `is_deleted = FALSE` | 外协单号唯一 |
| `idx_m10_lab_outsource_job` | B-Tree | `lab_job_id`, `step_seq` | 按加工单查外协 |
| `idx_m10_lab_outsource_open` | B-Tree（部分） | `expected_return_at`, `store_id` WHERE `status IN ('SENT','RECEIVED')` | **外协在途清单（P05）与逾期扫描** |
| `idx_m10_lab_outsource_supplier` | B-Tree | `supplier_code`, `sent_at` DESC | 供应商履约统计 |
| `idx_m10_lab_outsource_settle` | B-Tree（部分） | `supplier_code`, `outsource_fee` WHERE `fee_settle_status = 'UNSETTLED'` | 待结算外协费用 |

**约束说明：**

```sql
-- 外协单号格式：WX + 8 位日期 + 4 位序号
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_outsource
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_outsource_no_format
    CHECK (outsource_no ~ '^WX[0-9]{12}$');

-- 外协状态枚举（7 态）
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_outsource
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_outsource_status
    CHECK (status IN ('DRAFT', 'SENT', 'RECEIVED', 'INSPECTED',
                      'REJECTED', 'CLOSED', 'CANCELLED'));

-- 验收结果枚举
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_outsource
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_outsource_inspect
    CHECK (inspect_result IS NULL
           OR inspect_result IN ('PASS', 'FAIL', 'REWORK', 'RETURN_TO_SUPPLIER'));

-- 外协件缺陷类型枚举
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_outsource
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_outsource_defect
    CHECK (defect_type IS NULL
           OR defect_type IN ('SCRATCH', 'COATING_DEFECT', 'POWER_ERROR', 'OTHER'));

-- 结算状态枚举
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_outsource
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_outsource_settle
    CHECK (fee_settle_status IN ('UNSETTLED', 'SETTLED', 'VOID'));

-- 数量与费用非负
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_outsource
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_outsource_amount
    CHECK (qty > 0 AND outsource_fee >= 0 AND standard_return_days > 0
           AND return_times >= 0 AND overdue_days >= 0);

-- 承诺回片时刻必须晚于外送时刻
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_outsource
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_outsource_return_order
    CHECK (expected_return_at > sent_at);

-- 已回片必须有回片时刻
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_outsource
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_outsource_returned
    CHECK (status NOT IN ('RECEIVED', 'INSPECTED', 'REJECTED', 'CLOSED')
           OR returned_at IS NOT NULL);

-- 已验收必须有验收人、结果、时刻
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_outsource
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_outsource_inspected
    CHECK (status NOT IN ('INSPECTED', 'REJECTED')
           OR (inspect_result IS NOT NULL AND received_by IS NOT NULL));

-- 验收不合格必须有说明
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_outsource
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_outsource_reject_reason
    CHECK (inspect_result IS NULL OR inspect_result = 'PASS'
           OR (inspect_remark IS NOT NULL AND defect_type IS NOT NULL));

-- 已结算必须有结算参考号
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_outsource
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_outsource_settled
    CHECK (fee_settle_status <> 'SETTLED' OR settle_ref_no IS NOT NULL);

-- 取消必须有原因
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_outsource
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_outsource_cancelled
    CHECK (status <> 'CANCELLED' OR cancel_reason IS NOT NULL);

-- 逾期天数与逾期标记一致
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_outsource
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_outsource_overdue
    CHECK ((is_overdue = FALSE AND overdue_days = 0)
        OR (is_overdue = TRUE AND overdue_days > 0));

COMMENT ON TABLE m10_lab_outsource IS '外协加工单：外送、承诺回片、实际回片、验收、费用；对应加工单 OUTSOURCED 分支';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m10_lab_outsource.expected_return_at IS '承诺回片时刻 = sent_at + standard_return_days，逾期扫描与对客交期重算的依据';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m10_lab_outsource.return_times IS '外协退回重修次数，超过 2 次应更换供应商（见 8.4 校验规则）';
```

**外协状态机：**

```
     ┌─────────┐  确认外送  ┌────────┐  回片登记  ┌──────────┐  验收合格  ┌───────────┐
     │  DRAFT  │──────────►│  SENT  │──────────►│ RECEIVED │──────────►│ INSPECTED │
     │ 拟外送   │           │ 已外送  │           │ 已回片    │           │ 验收通过   │
     └────┬────┘           └───┬────┘           └────┬─────┘           └─────┬─────┘
          │ 取消                │ 取消                │ 验收不合格              │ 关闭
          ▼                     ▼                     ▼                      ▼
     ┌───────────┐        ┌───────────┐        ┌───────────┐          ┌────────┐
     │ CANCELLED │        │ CANCELLED │        │ REJECTED  │          │ CLOSED │
     │  已取消    │        │  已取消    │        │ 验收退回   │          │ 已关闭  │
     └───────────┘        └───────────┘        └─────┬─────┘          └────────┘
                                                     │ 重新外送（return_times+1）
                                                     ▼
                                              （回到 DRAFT → SENT）
```

**外协逾期如何影响交货承诺（本节关键）：**

```
【规则一：外协首次逾期 → 不影响 promised_at，但标记风险】
  expected_return_at 已过而 status 仍为 SENT：
    ① is_overdue=true，overdue_days 每日 +1
    ② 加工单发布 m10.lab.job.status_changed（附 risk='OUTSOURCE_OVERDUE'）
    ③ 加工看板（P01）该单标黄，加工主管可主动改期

【规则二：外协逾期 ≥ 3 天 → 强制重算并对客改期】
  若 overdue_days >= 3 且 (promised_at - now()) < 24 小时：
    ① planned_finish_at 按新的预计回片时刻重算
    ② 若新 planned_finish_at > promised_at + 24 小时 →
       触发"交期变更"，需加工主管确认后调 M10-06 改取镜预约
       （M10-07 不直接改订单，通过事件 m10.lab.promised_changed 通知）
    ③ 顾客通知由 M10-06 发送，M10-07 只提供新的对客时刻

【规则三：外协验收不合格（REJECTED）的工时计算】
  外协工序的 actual_minutes 不计入加工师工时（不是本院做的），
  但在 m10_lab_stat 中单列 outsource_minutes 用于成本核算；
  外协退回重做的第 2 次外送另建一张 outsource 单（return_times+1），
  不修改原单，保留完整的外送次数与外协成本。
```

---

### 4.12 表：`m10_lab_qc`（加工质检单表）

**用途：** 记录加工完成后的整单质检：检了哪几项、每项结果、不合格类型、判定结论（放行 / 返工 / 让步）、返工指向。

**质检项定义在 M01-08 工序属性的 `qc_items`，本表记录「实际检了什么、结果如何」。** 整单质检（本表）与工序质检（4.9 的 `qc_result`）是两层：工序质检由加工师自检，整单质检由独立质检员执行（职责分离）。

| 字段名 | 类型 | 允许空 | 默认 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | BIGINT | N | IDENTITY | 主键 |
| qc_no | VARCHAR(32) | N | — | 质检单号 `ZJ` + YYYYMMDD + 5 位 |
| lab_job_id | BIGINT | N | — | 加工单 ID |
| lab_job_no | VARCHAR(32) | N | — | 加工单号（冗余） |
| store_id | BIGINT | N | — | 门店 ID |
| qc_type | VARCHAR(16) | N | 'FINAL' | `FINAL` 整单终检 / `OUTSOURCE` 外协件验收 / `REWORK` 返工后复检 / `SPOT` 抽检 |
| qc_round | SMALLINT | N | 1 | 第几轮质检（同一加工单反复质检时递增） |
| **判定组** | | | | |
| qc_result | VARCHAR(16) | N | — | 质检结论 `PASS` 合格 / `FAIL` 不合格 |
| dispose_decision | VARCHAR(16) | Y | NULL | 处置决定 `RELEASE` 放行 / `REWORK` 返工 / `CONCESSION` 让步接受 / `SCRAP` 报废 / `RETURN_TO_SUPPLIER` 退回供应商 |
| fail_item_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 不合格项数 |
| fail_items | JSONB | Y | NULL | 不合格项明细数组 |
| defect_type | VARCHAR(24) | Y | NULL | 主缺陷类型（`qc_result='FAIL'` 必填） |
| defect_desc | VARCHAR(1024) | Y | NULL | 缺陷描述（人工填写，`qc_result='FAIL'` 必填） |
| qc_items | JSONB | N | '[]' | 全部质检项与结果 |
| **测量值组（关键质检项的实测值）** | | | | |
| measured_sph_od | NUMERIC(6,2) | Y | NULL | 实测右眼球镜，单位 D |
| measured_sph_os | NUMERIC(6,2) | Y | NULL | 实测左眼球镜，单位 D |
| measured_cyl_od | NUMERIC(6,2) | Y | NULL | 实测右眼柱镜，单位 D |
| measured_cyl_os | NUMERIC(6,2) | Y | NULL | 实测左眼柱镜，单位 D |
| measured_axis_od | SMALLINT | Y | NULL | 实测右眼轴位，单位 ° |
| measured_axis_os | SMALLINT | Y | NULL | 实测左眼轴位，单位 ° |
| measured_pd | NUMERIC(5,2) | Y | NULL | 实测瞳距，单位 mm |
| measured_pd_od | NUMERIC(5,2) | Y | NULL | 实测右眼单眼瞳距，单位 mm |
| measured_pd_os | NUMERIC(5,2) | Y | NULL | 实测左眼单眼瞳距，单位 mm |
| power_deviation_od | NUMERIC(5,2) | Y | NULL | 右眼光度偏差（实测 − 处方），单位 D |
| power_deviation_os | NUMERIC(5,2) | Y | NULL | 左眼光度偏差（实测 − 处方），单位 D |
| axis_deviation_od | SMALLINT | Y | NULL | 右眼轴位偏差，单位 ° |
| axis_deviation_os | SMALLINT | Y | NULL | 左眼轴位偏差，单位 ° |
| pd_deviation | NUMERIC(5,2) | Y | NULL | 瞳距偏差，单位 mm |
| **返工关联组** | | | | |
| rework_job_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 返工加工单 ID（`dispose_decision='REWORK'` 时必填） |
| rework_reason_code | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 返工原因代码（同 `defect_type`） |
| rework_chargeable | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 返工是否收费（加工师责任 → FALSE 院内承担；顾客改单 → TRUE） |
| rework_affects_promise | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 返工是否顺延交期承诺（见下方规则表） |
| new_promised_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 顺延后的新承诺时刻（`rework_affects_promise=true` 时必填） |
| concession_approval_no | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 让步接受审批单号（M20） |
| **执行组** | | | | |
| qc_by | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 质检员 ID |
| qc_by_name | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 质检员姓名（快照） |
| qc_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 质检时刻 |
| duration_seconds | INTEGER | Y | NULL | 质检耗时，单位秒 |
| **审计组** | | | | |
| remark | VARCHAR(1024) | Y | NULL | 备注 |
| created_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 创建时间 |
| updated_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 更新时间（触发器维护） |
| is_deleted | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 逻辑删除 |

**`qc_items` 元素结构与质检项定义：**

```json
[
  { "item_code": "QC_POWER",     "item_name": "度数核对",     "standard": "处方 ±0.12D", "measured": "-4.25", "deviation": 0.00,  "result": "PASS" },
  { "item_code": "QC_AXIS",      "item_name": "轴位核对",     "standard": "处方 ±3°",    "measured": 180,     "deviation": 0,     "result": "PASS" },
  { "item_code": "QC_OPTIC_CENTER", "item_name": "光学中心偏差", "standard": "≤ 2.5mm",  "measured": 1.2,     "deviation": 0.0,   "result": "PASS" },
  { "item_code": "QC_SURFACE",   "item_name": "镜片表面瑕疵", "standard": "无划伤/气泡/杂质", "measured": "合格", "deviation": 0.0, "result": "PASS" },
  { "item_code": "QC_MOUNT",     "item_name": "装配合格",     "standard": "无松动/无缝隙/开合顺畅", "measured": "合格", "deviation": 0.0, "result": "PASS" },
  { "item_code": "QC_FRAME_SYM", "item_name": "镜架对称性",   "standard": "左右对称偏差 ≤ 1°", "measured": 0.4,   "deviation": 0.4, "result": "PASS" }
]
```

**索引设计：**

| 索引名 | 类型 | 字段 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `pk_m10_lab_qc` | 主键 | `id` | — |
| `uk_m10_lab_qc_no` | 唯一（部分） | `qc_no` WHERE `is_deleted = FALSE` | 质检单号唯一 |
| `uk_m10_lab_qc_round` | 唯一（部分） | `lab_job_id`, `qc_round`, `qc_type` WHERE `is_deleted = FALSE` | 同一加工单同一轮次唯一 |
| `idx_m10_lab_qc_job` | B-Tree | `lab_job_id`, `qc_at` DESC | 按加工单查质检历史 |
| `idx_m10_lab_qc_fail` | B-Tree（部分） | `defect_type`, `qc_at` DESC WHERE `qc_result = 'FAIL'` | **不合格分析（P09 质量报表）** |
| `idx_m10_lab_qc_rework` | B-Tree（部分） | `rework_job_id` WHERE `rework_job_id IS NOT NULL` | 返工追溯 |
| `idx_m10_lab_qc_concession` | B-Tree（部分） | `qc_at` WHERE `dispose_decision = 'CONCESSION'` | 让步接受专项审计 |
| `idx_m10_lab_qc_qcby` | B-Tree | `qc_by`, `qc_at` DESC | 质检员工作量与合格率 |

**约束说明：**

```sql
-- 质检单号格式：ZJ + 8 位日期 + 5 位序号
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_qc
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_qc_no_format
    CHECK (qc_no ~ '^ZJ[0-9]{13}$');

-- 质检类型枚举
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_qc
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_qc_type
    CHECK (qc_type IN ('FINAL', 'OUTSOURCE', 'REWORK', 'SPOT'));

-- 质检结论枚举
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_qc
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_qc_result
    CHECK (qc_result IN ('PASS', 'FAIL'));

-- 处置决定枚举
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_qc
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_qc_decision
    CHECK (dispose_decision IS NULL
           OR dispose_decision IN ('RELEASE', 'REWORK', 'CONCESSION',
                                   'SCRAP', 'RETURN_TO_SUPPLIER'));

-- 缺陷类型枚举
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_qc
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_qc_defect
    CHECK (defect_type IS NULL
           OR defect_type IN ('POWER_ERROR', 'AXIS_ERROR', 'MOUNT_OFFSET',
                              'SCRATCH', 'POWER_MISMATCH', 'FRAME_ASYMMETRY'));

-- 轮次与项数非负
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_qc
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_qc_numeric
    CHECK (qc_round >= 1 AND fail_item_count >= 0
           AND (duration_seconds IS NULL OR duration_seconds >= 0));

-- 质检项必须是 JSON 数组
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_qc
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_qc_items_array
    CHECK (jsonb_typeof(qc_items) = 'array');

-- 不合格项明细必须是数组（合格时可为 NULL）
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_qc
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_qc_fail_items
    CHECK (fail_items IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(fail_items) = 'array');

-- 不合格必须有缺陷类型、描述、不合格项数 > 0
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_qc
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_qc_failed
    CHECK (qc_result <> 'FAIL'
           OR (defect_type IS NOT NULL AND defect_desc IS NOT NULL
               AND fail_item_count > 0 AND fail_items IS NOT NULL));

-- 合格时不允许有不合格项
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_qc
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_qc_passed
    CHECK (qc_result <> 'PASS' OR fail_item_count = 0);

-- 处置决定与质检结论一致性：合格只能 RELEASE；不合格不能 RELEASE
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_qc
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_qc_decision_consistency
    CHECK ((qc_result = 'PASS' AND (dispose_decision IS NULL OR dispose_decision = 'RELEASE'))
        OR (qc_result = 'FAIL' AND dispose_decision IN ('REWORK', 'CONCESSION',
                                                        'SCRAP', 'RETURN_TO_SUPPLIER')));

-- 返工必须有返工单号与原因
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_qc
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_qc_rework
    CHECK (dispose_decision <> 'REWORK'
           OR (rework_job_id IS NOT NULL AND rework_reason_code IS NOT NULL));

-- 顺延交期必须有新承诺时刻
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_qc
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_qc_promise
    CHECK (rework_affects_promise = FALSE OR new_promised_at IS NOT NULL);

-- 让步接受必须有审批单号
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_qc
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_qc_concession
    CHECK (dispose_decision <> 'CONCESSION' OR concession_approval_no IS NOT NULL);

-- 质检人必须有
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_qc
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_qc_by
    CHECK (qc_by IS NOT NULL AND qc_by_name IS NOT NULL);

COMMENT ON TABLE m10_lab_qc IS '加工质检单：整单终检与外协验收记录，含质检项 JSONB、实测值、缺陷分类、返工与让步判定';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m10_lab_qc.qc_items IS '质检项与结果数组，元素含 item_code/item_name/standard/measured/deviation/result';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m10_lab_qc.rework_affects_promise IS '返工是否顺延对客交期承诺：加工师责任→TRUE（院内承担，不顺延）；顾客改单→TRUE（顺延）；轻微缺陷让步→FALSE';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m10_lab_qc.new_promised_at IS '顺延后的新对客承诺时刻，由 M10-06 据此调整取镜预约';
```

**返工是否重计工时 / 是否影响交期承诺（规则表）：**

| 场景 | 责任方 | 返工是否重计工时 | 是否顺延对客承诺 | 返工是否收费 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 度数错（`POWER_ERROR`） | 加工师 | **是**（新单独立计时） | **否**（院内承担，承诺不变） | 否 | 交期已承诺，医院内部消化 |
| 轴位错（`AXIS_ERROR`） | 加工师 | 是 | 否 | 否 | 同上 |
| 装配偏移（`MOUNT_OFFSET`） | 加工师 | 是 | 否 | 否 | 同上 |
| 镜片划伤（`SCRATCH`） | 加工师 或 供应商 | 是（本院重做部分） | 否 | 否 | 若为外协件划伤由供应商赔付 |
| 光度与处方不符（`POWER_MISMATCH`） | 厂家来片错 | 是 | **是**（等新片，需对客改期） | 否 | 需重新向厂家下单，`lead_time_days` 重算 |
| 镜架不对称（`FRAME_ASYMMETRY`） | 加工师 | 是 | 否 | 否 | 可现场调整，通常不换片 |
| 顾客主动改参数（非质检失败） | 顾客 | 是 | **是** | **是**（按 M16 改单收费） | 走 M10-06 改单流程，非本表负面事件 |
| 轻微外观瑕疵顾客接受 | — | 否 | 否 | 否 | `CONCESSION` 让步，需店长审批 |

> **工时口径关键说明：** 返工**不修改原加工单与原工序的 `actual_minutes`**。原工序的实际工时如实记录（加工师 A 花了 18 分钟磨错），返工单的新工序单独记录（加工师 B 花了 15 分钟重磨）。`m10_lab_stat` 中分别统计 `original_minutes`（首次加工工时）与 `rework_minutes`（返工工时），合格率与效率考核使用首次加工工时作为分母，返工工时单列为质量成本。

**量级估算：**

| 项目 | 数值 |
|---|---|
| 年质检单数 | 约 19,800 张（18,000 首次 + 900 返工复检 + 900 外协验收） |
| 3 年累计 | 约 59,400 行 |
| 单行平均长度 | 约 1,400 字节（含两个 JSONB） |
| 表大小（3 年） | 约 83 MB |
| 索引大小（3 年） | 约 30 MB |
| 日均写入 | 约 55 INSERT |

---

### 4.13 表：`m10_lab_stat`（加工与库存统计表）

**用途：** 按「日期 × 门店 × 加工师」与「日期 × 门店 × 品类」两个维度预聚合的日统计。加工产能报表（P09）、库存周转报表（P07）与 M22 报表取数都读本表，避免每次全表扫 4.9 的 39 万行。

| 字段名 | 类型 | 允许空 | 默认 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | BIGINT | N | IDENTITY | 主键 |
| stat_date | DATE | N | — | 统计日期 |
| store_id | BIGINT | N | — | 门店 ID |
| store_code | VARCHAR(32) | N | — | 门店编码（快照） |
| stat_type | VARCHAR(16) | N | 'OPERATOR' | 统计维度 `OPERATOR` 加工师 / `KIND` 品类 / `STORE` 门店整体 |
| operator_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 加工师 ID（`stat_type='OPERATOR'` 时必填） |
| operator_name | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 加工师姓名（快照） |
| product_kind | VARCHAR(16) | Y | NULL | 品类（`stat_type='KIND'` 时必填） |
| **加工量组** | | | | |
| job_created_count | INTEGER | N | 0 | 当日建单数 |
| job_completed_count | INTEGER | N | 0 | 当日完工数 |
| job_cancelled_count | INTEGER | N | 0 | 当日取消数 |
| job_rework_count | INTEGER | N | 0 | 当日返工单数 |
| step_completed_count | INTEGER | N | 0 | 当日完成工序数 |
| job_overdue_count | INTEGER | N | 0 | 当日逾期单数 |
| job_outsource_count | INTEGER | N | 0 | 当日外协单数 |
| **工时组** | | | | |
| original_minutes | INTEGER | N | 0 | 首次加工工时合计，单位分钟 |
| rework_minutes | INTEGER | N | 0 | 返工工时合计，单位分钟 |
| outsource_minutes | INTEGER | N | 0 | 外协工序标准工时合计（不在本院消耗的工时） |
| standard_minutes | INTEGER | N | 0 | 标准工时合计，单位分钟 |
| idle_minutes | INTEGER | N | 0 | 空闲工时（班次时长 − 实际工时），单位分钟 |
| capacity_minutes | INTEGER | N | 0 | 当日可用产能，单位分钟 |
| overtime_minutes | INTEGER | N | 0 | 加班工时，单位分钟 |
| **质量组** | | | | |
| qc_count | INTEGER | N | 0 | 当日质检单数 |
| qc_pass_count | INTEGER | N | 0 | 质检合格数 |
| qc_fail_count | INTEGER | N | 0 | 质检不合格数 |
| concession_count | INTEGER | N | 0 | 让步接受数 |
| scrap_count | INTEGER | N | 0 | 报废数 |
| on_time_count | INTEGER | N | 0 | 按期交付数 |
| **库存组（`stat_type='STORE'` 时写）** | | | | |
| sku_count | INTEGER | N | 0 | 在架 SKU 数 |
| batch_count | INTEGER | N | 0 | 在架批次数 |
| qty_on_hand | NUMERIC(14,4) | N | 0 | 期末在架量合计 |
| qty_reserved | NUMERIC(14,4) | N | 0 | 期末有效预占量合计 |
| qty_below_safety | INTEGER | N | 0 | 低于安全库存的 SKU 数 |
| stock_amount | NUMERIC(14,2) | N | 0 | 期末库存金额，单位元 |
| in_qty | NUMERIC(14,4) | N | 0 | 当日入库量合计 |
| out_qty | NUMERIC(14,4) | N | 0 | 当日出库量合计 |
| turnover_rate | NUMERIC(8,4) | N | 0 | 库存周转率 = 当日出库量 / 平均在架量 |
| expiry_alert_count | INTEGER | N | 0 | 近效期批次告警数 |
| reconcile_diff_count | INTEGER | N | 0 | 当日对账差异行数 |
| **非生成列指标（用生成列表达比值）** | | | | |
| pass_rate | NUMERIC(6,4) | N | — | 质检合格率（生成列）= qc_pass / qc_count |
| on_time_rate | NUMERIC(6,4) | N | — | 按期率（生成列）= on_time / job_completed |
| capacity_utilization | NUMERIC(6,4) | N | — | 产能利用率（生成列）= (original + rework) / capacity |
| **审计组** | | | | |
| calculated_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 统计计算时刻 |
| calc_version | SMALLINT | N | 1 | 重算版本（同日重跑递增，取最新版） |
| created_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 创建时间 |
| updated_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 更新时间（触发器维护） |

**索引设计：**

| 索引名 | 类型 | 字段 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `pk_m10_lab_stat` | 主键 | `id` | — |
| `uk_m10_lab_stat_key` | 唯一 | `stat_date`, `store_id`, `stat_type`, `operator_id`, `product_kind` | 同日同维度唯一（空值用 0 占位，避免 NULL 破坏唯一性） |
| `idx_m10_lab_stat_date` | B-Tree | `stat_date` DESC, `store_id` | 日报与区间报表 |
| `idx_m10_lab_stat_operator` | B-Tree（部分） | `operator_id`, `stat_date` DESC WHERE `stat_type = 'OPERATOR'` | 加工师产能报表（P09） |
| `idx_m10_lab_stat_kind` | B-Tree（部分） | `product_kind`, `stat_date` DESC WHERE `stat_type = 'KIND'` | 品类结构分析 |
| `idx_m10_lab_stat_store` | B-Tree（部分） | `store_id`, `stat_date` DESC WHERE `stat_type = 'STORE'` | 门店经营与库存周转 |

**约束说明：**

```sql
-- 统计维度枚举
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_stat
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_stat_type
    CHECK (stat_type IN ('OPERATOR', 'KIND', 'STORE'));

-- 维度字段与 stat_type 对应（不允许 OPERATOR 维度缺加工师）
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_stat
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_stat_dims
    CHECK ((stat_type = 'OPERATOR' AND operator_id IS NOT NULL AND product_kind IS NULL)
        OR (stat_type = 'KIND'     AND product_kind IS NOT NULL AND operator_id IS NULL)
        OR (stat_type = 'STORE'    AND operator_id IS NULL AND product_kind IS NULL));

-- 品类枚举
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_stat
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_stat_kind
    CHECK (product_kind IS NULL
           OR product_kind IN ('FRAME', 'LENS', 'CONTACT', 'ORTHO_K', 'RGP',
                               'CARE', 'SUN', 'ACCESSORY', 'SERVICE'));

-- 各类计数非负
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_stat
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_stat_counts
    CHECK (job_created_count >= 0 AND job_completed_count >= 0 AND job_cancelled_count >= 0
       AND job_rework_count >= 0 AND step_completed_count >= 0 AND job_overdue_count >= 0
       AND job_outsource_count >= 0 AND qc_count >= 0 AND qc_pass_count >= 0
       AND qc_fail_count >= 0 AND concession_count >= 0 AND scrap_count >= 0
       AND on_time_count >= 0 AND sku_count >= 0 AND batch_count >= 0
       AND qty_below_safety >= 0 AND expiry_alert_count >= 0 AND reconcile_diff_count >= 0);

-- 工时类非负
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_stat
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_stat_minutes
    CHECK (original_minutes >= 0 AND rework_minutes >= 0 AND outsource_minutes >= 0
       AND standard_minutes >= 0 AND idle_minutes >= 0 AND capacity_minutes >= 0
       AND overtime_minutes >= 0);

-- 数量与金额非负
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_stat
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_stat_qty
    CHECK (qty_on_hand >= 0 AND qty_reserved >= 0 AND stock_amount >= 0
       AND in_qty >= 0 AND out_qty >= 0 AND turnover_rate >= 0);

-- 合格数 ≤ 质检数；按期数 ≤ 完工数
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_stat
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_stat_ratio_bound
    CHECK (qc_pass_count + qc_fail_count <= qc_count
       AND on_time_count <= job_completed_count);

-- 三个比率为生成列（分母为 0 时返回 0，避免 NULL 传播）
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_stat
    ADD COLUMN pass_rate NUMERIC(6,4)
    GENERATED ALWAYS AS (CASE WHEN qc_count > 0
                              THEN ROUND(qc_pass_count::NUMERIC / qc_count, 4) ELSE 0 END) STORED;

ALTER TABLE m10_lab_stat
    ADD COLUMN on_time_rate NUMERIC(6,4)
    GENERATED ALWAYS AS (CASE WHEN job_completed_count > 0
                              THEN ROUND(on_time_count::NUMERIC / job_completed_count, 4) ELSE 0 END) STORED;

ALTER TABLE m10_lab_stat
    ADD COLUMN capacity_utilization NUMERIC(6,4)
    GENERATED ALWAYS AS (CASE WHEN capacity_minutes > 0
                              THEN ROUND((original_minutes + rework_minutes)::NUMERIC
                                         / capacity_minutes, 4) ELSE 0 END) STORED;

-- 重算版本从 1 起
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_stat
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_stat_calc
    CHECK (calc_version >= 1);

COMMENT ON TABLE m10_lab_stat IS '加工与库存日统计：按 日期×门店×加工师 / 日期×门店×品类 / 日期×门店 三个维度预聚合，供 P09 与 M22 取数';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m10_lab_stat.original_minutes IS '首次加工工时（返工不计入），作为效率考核分母';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m10_lab_stat.rework_minutes IS '返工工时，单列为质量成本，不参与效率考核';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m10_lab_stat.capacity_utilization IS '生成列：(首次工时 + 返工工时) / 可用产能，反映加工师饱和程度';
```

**量级估算：**

| 项目 | 数值 |
|---|---|
| 维度组合 | OPERATOR：4 门店 × 平均 5 名加工师 = 20 行/日；KIND：4 × 8 品类 = 32 行/日；STORE：4 行/日 |
| 日行数 | 约 56 行 |
| 年行数 | 约 20,400 行 |
| 3 年累计 | 约 61,000 行 |
| 单行平均长度 | 约 480 字节 |
| 表大小（3 年） | 约 29 MB |
| 分区 | 不分区（3 年 6 万行） |

---

### 4.14 SQLAlchemy 2.0 模型定义

> 全部 12 张表一一对应一个 `class`，保留 `__table_args__` 中的索引与 CHECK 声明。字段顺序与 4.2~4.13 的字段表顺序一致，便于逐列核对。

```python
"""M10-07 视光库存与加工作业 —— SQLAlchemy 2.0 模型"""

from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from sqlalchemy import (
    BigInteger, Boolean, CheckConstraint, Date, DateTime, Identity,
    Index, Integer, Numeric, SmallInteger, String, Text, UniqueConstraint,
    func, text,
)
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import JSONB
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from app.models.base import Base


# ==================================================================
# 库存域：m10_stock_*
# ==================================================================


class M10StockBatch(Base):
    """门店二级库批次台账表。

    记录视光中心门店在架的每一批商品，是 P-18 可销售量查询的数据基础。
    qty_on_hand 只是本门店货架上的实物量，全院库存权威在 M15-01 m15_stock。
    """

    __tablename__ = "m10_stock_batch"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    batch_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(96), nullable=False)
    store_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    store_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    store_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=False)
    warehouse_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    warehouse_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)

    # 商品与 SKU
    product_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    product_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    product_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), nullable=False)
    product_kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)
    sku_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(96), nullable=False)
    sphere: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    cylinder: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    axis: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    color_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    unit: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="副")

    # 批次与效期
    batch_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    production_date: Mapped[Optional[date]] = mapped_column(Date)
    expiry_date: Mapped[Optional[date]] = mapped_column(Date)
    issue_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    issue_line_no: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer)
    supply_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="STOCK")
    lead_time_days: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)

    # 数量（qty_available 与 amount 为数据库生成列，ORM 侧只读）
    qty_on_hand: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                                                 default=Decimal("0"))
    qty_safety: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                                                default=Decimal("0"))
    qty_reserved: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                                                  default=Decimal("0"))
    qty_available: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(12, 4),
        server_default=text("GREATEST(qty_on_hand - qty_reserved - qty_safety, 0)"),
        computed=text("GREATEST(qty_on_hand - qty_reserved - qty_safety, 0)"),
        nullable=False,
    )
    unit_cost: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                                               default=Decimal("0"))
    amount: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 2),
        computed=text("ROUND(qty_on_hand * unit_cost, 2)"),
        nullable=False,
    )

    # 状态与审计
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="AVAILABLE")
    last_in_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    last_out_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=1)
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False,
                                                 server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False,
        server_default=func.current_timestamp(), onupdate=func.current_timestamp(),
    )
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m10_stock_batch_key", "batch_key", unique=True),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_batch_sku", "store_id", "sku_key",
              postgresql_where=text("status = 'AVAILABLE' AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_batch_store", "store_id", "product_kind",
              text("updated_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_batch_product", "product_code", "store_id"),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_batch_issue", "issue_no",
              postgresql_where=text("issue_no IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_batch_expiry", "expiry_date",
              postgresql_where=text("expiry_date IS NOT NULL "
                                    "AND status IN ('AVAILABLE','LOCKED')")),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_batch_low", "store_id", "sku_key",
              postgresql_where=text("qty_on_hand <= qty_safety AND status = 'AVAILABLE'")),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_batch_name_trgm", "product_name",
              postgresql_using="gin", postgresql_ops={"product_name": "gin_trgm_ops"}),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_batch_warehouse", "warehouse_code", "product_code"),
        CheckConstraint("status IN ('AVAILABLE','LOCKED','DEPLETED','EXPIRED',"
                        "'DAMAGED','FROZEN')", name="ck_m10_stock_batch_status"),
        CheckConstraint("product_kind IN ('FRAME','LENS','CONTACT','ORTHO_K','RGP',"
                        "'CARE','SUN','ACCESSORY','SERVICE')",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_batch_kind"),
        CheckConstraint("supply_type IN ('STOCK','CUSTOM')",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_batch_supply"),
        CheckConstraint("supply_type <> 'CUSTOM' OR (lead_time_days IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND lead_time_days > 0)", name="ck_m10_stock_batch_lead"),
        CheckConstraint("qty_on_hand >= 0 AND qty_safety >= 0 AND qty_reserved >= 0",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_batch_qty_nonneg"),
        CheckConstraint("qty_reserved <= qty_on_hand", name="ck_m10_stock_batch_qty_bound"),
        CheckConstraint("sphere IS NULL OR (sphere >= -30.00 AND sphere <= 30.00 "
                        "AND (sphere * 4) = trunc(sphere * 4))",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_batch_sph_step"),
        CheckConstraint("cylinder IS NULL OR (cylinder >= -8.00 AND cylinder <= 8.00 "
                        "AND (cylinder * 4) = trunc(cylinder * 4))",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_batch_cyl_step"),
        CheckConstraint("(axis IS NULL AND (cylinder IS NULL OR cylinder = 0)) "
                        "OR (axis BETWEEN 1 AND 180 AND cylinder <> 0)",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_batch_axis"),
        CheckConstraint("status <> 'DEPLETED' OR qty_on_hand = 0",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_batch_depleted"),
        CheckConstraint("status NOT IN ('EXPIRED','DAMAGED') OR last_out_at IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_batch_invalid"),
        CheckConstraint("last_out_at IS NULL OR last_in_at IS NULL "
                        "OR last_out_at >= last_in_at", name="ck_m10_stock_batch_time"),
        CheckConstraint("version >= 1", name="ck_m10_stock_batch_version"),
    )


class M10StockReserve(Base):
    """库存预占表。

    下单即预占、付款转出库、退单释放。部分唯一索引
    (sku_key, source_no) WHERE status='ACTIVE' 从数据库层杜绝重复预占，
    两段式事务（预占 → 出库确认）防止超卖。
    """

    __tablename__ = "m10_stock_reserve"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    reserve_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    store_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    batch_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    batch_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(96), nullable=False)
    sku_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(96), nullable=False)
    product_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    product_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), nullable=False)

    # 来源单据
    source_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=False)
    source_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    source_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    source_item_seq: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    sales_order_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))

    # 数量与状态
    qty_reserved: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                                                  default=Decimal("1"))
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="ACTIVE")
    reserved_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False,
                                                  server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    expires_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False)
    released_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    release_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    consumed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    consumed_movement_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)

    # 幂等与审计
    idempotency_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=False)
    operator_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False,
                                                 server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False,
        server_default=func.current_timestamp(), onupdate=func.current_timestamp(),
    )
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m10_stock_reserve_no", "reserve_no", unique=True),
        Index("uk_m10_stock_reserve_idem", "idempotency_key", unique=True),
        Index("uk_m10_stock_reserve_active", "sku_key", "source_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("status = 'ACTIVE'")),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_reserve_batch", "batch_id",
              postgresql_where=text("status = 'ACTIVE'")),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_reserve_source", "source_type", "source_no"),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_reserve_expire", "expires_at",
              postgresql_where=text("status = 'ACTIVE'")),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_reserve_store", "store_id", text("reserved_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_reserve_status", "status", text("reserved_at DESC")),
        CheckConstraint("reserve_no ~ '^YZ[0-9]{14}$'",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_reserve_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint("status IN ('ACTIVE','CONSUMED','RELEASED','EXPIRED','CANCELLED')",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_reserve_status"),
        CheckConstraint("source_type IN ('SALES_ORDER','LAB_JOB','MANUAL','REWORK')",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_reserve_source_type"),
        CheckConstraint("release_reason IS NULL OR release_reason IN "
                        "('ORDER_CANCELLED','TTL_EXPIRED','SHORTAGE','MANUAL','REFUNDED')",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_reserve_reason"),
        CheckConstraint("qty_reserved > 0", name="ck_m10_stock_reserve_qty"),
        CheckConstraint("expires_at > reserved_at", name="ck_m10_stock_reserve_expire_order"),
        CheckConstraint("status = 'ACTIVE' "
                        "OR (status = 'CONSUMED' AND consumed_at IS NOT NULL) "
                        "OR (status IN ('RELEASED','EXPIRED','CANCELLED') "
                        "AND released_at IS NOT NULL AND release_reason IS NOT NULL)",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_reserve_closed"),
        CheckConstraint("status <> 'CONSUMED' OR consumed_movement_id IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_reserve_consumed"),
        CheckConstraint("expires_at <= reserved_at + INTERVAL '7 days'",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_reserve_ttl"),
    )


class M10StockMovement(Base):
    """门店库出入库流水表（按 created_at 月度分区）。

    只增不改：任何更正只能追加一条 ADJUST_REVERSE 反向流水，
    不允许 UPDATE / DELETE，保证审计证据完整。
    """

    __tablename__ = "m10_stock_movement"
    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m10_stock_movement_no", "movement_no", "created_at", unique=True),
        Index("uk_m10_stock_movement_idem", "idempotency_key", "created_at", unique=True),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_movement_batch", "batch_id", text("occurred_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_movement_store", "store_id", text("occurred_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_movement_source", "source_type", "source_no"),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_movement_day", "occurred_at"),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_movement_sku", "sku_key", text("occurred_at DESC")),
        CheckConstraint("movement_no ~ '^LS[0-9]{16}$'",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_movement_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint("txn_type IN ('IN_ISSUE','IN_RETURN','IN_CHECK','OUT_SALE',"
                        "'OUT_LAB','OUT_CHECK','OUT_SCRAP','ADJUST_REVERSE')",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_movement_type"),
        CheckConstraint("direction IN ('IN','OUT')", name="ck_m10_stock_movement_direction"),
        CheckConstraint("qty > 0", name="ck_m10_stock_movement_qty_positive"),
        CheckConstraint("(direction = 'IN' AND after_qty = before_qty + qty) "
                        "OR (direction = 'OUT' AND after_qty = before_qty - qty)",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_movement_balance"),
        CheckConstraint("after_qty >= 0 AND before_qty >= 0",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_movement_nonneg"),
        CheckConstraint("txn_type <> 'OUT_SALE' OR reserve_no IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_movement_reserve"),
        CheckConstraint("source_type IN ('ISSUE','SALES_ORDER','LAB_JOB',"
                        "'STOCK_CHECK','SCRAP','RETURN')",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_movement_source"),
        {"postgresql_partition_by": "RANGE (created_at)"},
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    movement_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    store_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    batch_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    batch_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(96), nullable=False)
    sku_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(96), nullable=False)
    product_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    product_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), nullable=False)

    # 事务
    txn_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=False)
    direction: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=False)
    qty: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False)
    before_qty: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False)
    after_qty: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False)
    unit_cost: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                                               default=Decimal("0"))
    total_cost: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 2), computed=text("ROUND(qty * unit_cost, 2)"), nullable=False,
    )

    # 来源
    source_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=False)
    source_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    source_line_no: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer)
    reserve_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))

    # 审计
    idempotency_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=False)
    occurred_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False,
                                                  server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    operator_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    operator_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False,
                                                 server_default=func.current_timestamp())


class M10StockCheck(Base):
    """门店二级库盘点单表（明细以 JSONB detail_lines 承载）。

    盘亏金额超阈值需 M20 审批；差异确认后回传 M15（M10 不直接改主库）。
    """

    __tablename__ = "m10_stock_check"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    check_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    store_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    store_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    check_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="MONTHLY")
    check_scope: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=False, default="ALL")
    scope_filter: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)

    # 时间
    check_date: Mapped[date] = mapped_column(Date, nullable=False)
    started_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    submitted_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    confirmed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)

    # 数量汇总
    line_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    book_qty_total: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(14, 4), nullable=False,
                                                    default=Decimal("0"))
    actual_qty_total: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(14, 4), nullable=False,
                                                      default=Decimal("0"))
    gain_qty_total: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(14, 4), nullable=False,
                                                    default=Decimal("0"))
    loss_qty_total: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(14, 4), nullable=False,
                                                    default=Decimal("0"))
    gain_amount: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(14, 2), nullable=False,
                                                 default=Decimal("0"))
    loss_amount: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(14, 2), nullable=False,
                                                 default=Decimal("0"))
    diff_line_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)

    # 明细
    detail_lines: Mapped[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=False,
                                                              default=list)

    # 状态与回传
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="DRAFT")
    approval_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    approval_status: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))
    m15_callback_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False,
                                                     default="PENDING")
    m15_callback_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    m15_callback_retry: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0)

    # 审计
    checker_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    checker_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    reviewer_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    reviewer_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    confirmed_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    cancel_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1024))
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=1)
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False,
                                                 server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False,
        server_default=func.current_timestamp(), onupdate=func.current_timestamp(),
    )
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m10_stock_check_no", "check_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_check_store", "store_id", text("check_date DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_check_status", "store_id", "status",
              postgresql_where=text("status IN ('DRAFT','CHECKING','SUBMITTED')")),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_check_callback", "m15_callback_status",
              postgresql_where=text("m15_callback_status IN ('PENDING','FAILED')")),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_check_lines", "detail_lines",
              postgresql_using="gin", postgresql_ops={"detail_lines": "jsonb_path_ops"}),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_check_date", "check_date"),
        CheckConstraint("check_no ~ '^PD[0-9]{11}$'", name="ck_m10_stock_check_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint("check_type IN ('MONTHLY','SPOT','FULL','SPECIAL')",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_check_type"),
        CheckConstraint("check_scope IN ('ALL','KIND','SKU','BATCH')",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_check_scope"),
        CheckConstraint("status IN ('DRAFT','CHECKING','SUBMITTED','CONFIRMED',"
                        "'REJECTED','CANCELLED')", name="ck_m10_stock_check_status"),
        CheckConstraint("m15_callback_status IN ('PENDING','SUCCESS','FAILED','SKIPPED')",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_check_cb"),
        CheckConstraint("jsonb_typeof(detail_lines) = 'array'",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_check_lines_array"),
        CheckConstraint("line_count >= 0 AND diff_line_count >= 0 "
                        "AND diff_line_count <= line_count",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_check_line_count"),
        CheckConstraint("gain_amount >= 0 AND loss_amount >= 0 "
                        "AND gain_qty_total >= 0 AND loss_qty_total >= 0",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_check_amount"),
        CheckConstraint("status <> 'CONFIRMED' OR (confirmed_by IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND confirmed_at IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND m15_callback_status <> 'PENDING')",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_check_confirmed"),
        CheckConstraint("status <> 'CANCELLED' OR (cancel_reason IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND char_length(btrim(cancel_reason)) >= 5)",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_check_cancelled"),
        CheckConstraint("loss_amount <= 500.00 OR (approval_no IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND approval_status = 'APPROVED')",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_check_approval"),
        CheckConstraint("(submitted_at IS NULL OR started_at IS NULL "
                        "OR submitted_at >= started_at) AND (confirmed_at IS NULL "
                        "OR submitted_at IS NULL OR confirmed_at >= submitted_at)",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_check_time"),
    )


class M10StockReconcile(Base):
    """库存对账差异表：每日比对 M10 门店二级库汇总与 M15 主库台账。"""

    __tablename__ = "m10_stock_reconcile"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    reconcile_date: Mapped[date] = mapped_column(Date, nullable=False)
    reconcile_batch_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    store_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    warehouse_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    sku_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(96), nullable=False)
    batch_key: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(96))
    product_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    product_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), nullable=False)
    reconcile_level: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="SKU")

    # 两侧数量
    m10_qty_on_hand: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                                                     default=Decimal("0"))
    m10_qty_reserved: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                                                      default=Decimal("0"))
    m15_qty_on_hand: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                                                     default=Decimal("0"))
    m15_qty_locked: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                                                    default=Decimal("0"))
    diff_qty: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(12, 4), computed=text("m10_qty_on_hand - m15_qty_on_hand"), nullable=False,
    )
    diff_amount: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 2),
        computed=text("ROUND((m10_qty_on_hand - m15_qty_on_hand) * unit_cost, 2)"),
        nullable=False,
    )
    unit_cost: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                                               default=Decimal("0"))

    # 差异定性
    diff_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=False, default="MATCHED")
    tolerance_qty: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                                                   default=Decimal("0"))
    is_beyond_tolerance: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    root_cause: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    handle_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="OPEN")
    handle_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    handle_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    handle_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    handle_remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))

    # 审计
    m15_snapshot_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    trace_id: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False,
                                                 server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False,
        server_default=func.current_timestamp(), onupdate=func.current_timestamp(),
    )
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m10_stock_reconcile_key", "reconcile_date", "reconcile_batch_no",
              "warehouse_code", "sku_key", "reconcile_level", unique=True),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_reconcile_open", "handle_status",
              text("reconcile_date DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("handle_status IN ('OPEN','HANDLING')")),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_reconcile_beyond", "reconcile_date", "store_id",
              postgresql_where=text("is_beyond_tolerance = TRUE")),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_reconcile_date", "reconcile_date", "store_id"),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_reconcile_sku", "sku_key", text("reconcile_date DESC")),
        CheckConstraint("diff_type IN ('MATCHED','M10_MORE','M15_MORE','RESERVE_DIFF',"
                        "'MISSING_M10','MISSING_M15')", name="ck_m10_stock_reconcile_type"),
        CheckConstraint("reconcile_level IN ('SKU','BATCH','STORE')",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_reconcile_level"),
        CheckConstraint("handle_status IN ('OPEN','HANDLING','RESOLVED','IGNORED')",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_reconcile_handle"),
        CheckConstraint("root_cause IS NULL OR root_cause IN ('UNSYNCED_ISSUE',"
                        "'UNSYNCED_SALE','TIMING','DATA_ERROR','M15_ADJUSTED','UNKNOWN')",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_reconcile_cause"),
        CheckConstraint("diff_type <> 'MATCHED' OR m10_qty_on_hand = m15_qty_on_hand",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_reconcile_matched"),
        CheckConstraint("is_beyond_tolerance = FALSE OR root_cause IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_reconcile_cause_required"),
        CheckConstraint("handle_status <> 'RESOLVED' OR (handle_type IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND handle_by IS NOT NULL AND handle_at IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND handle_remark IS NOT NULL)",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_reconcile_resolved"),
        CheckConstraint("tolerance_qty >= 0", name="ck_m10_stock_reconcile_tolerance"),
    )


class M10StockPolicy(Base):
    """视光库存策略表：安全库存、补货点、预占 TTL、对账容差（KIND→PRODUCT→SKU 三级继承）。"""

    __tablename__ = "m10_stock_policy"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    store_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    store_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    product_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    sku_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(96), nullable=False)
    product_kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)
    policy_level: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="SKU")

    # 库存策略
    qty_safety: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                                                default=Decimal("1"))
    qty_reorder_point: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                                                       default=Decimal("2"))
    qty_max: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(12, 4))
    qty_min_display: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                                                     default=Decimal("0"))

    # 预占策略
    reserve_ttl_minutes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=1440)
    reserve_ttl_urgent_minutes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False,
                                                            default=4320)
    reserve_ttl_custom_minutes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False,
                                                            default=10080)
    allow_oversell: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    oversell_limit: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                                                    default=Decimal("0"))

    # 盘点与容差
    reconcile_tolerance: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                                                         default=Decimal("0"))
    check_cycle_days: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=30)
    approval_loss_amount: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(14, 2), nullable=False,
                                                          default=Decimal("500.00"))

    # 预警
    expiry_alert_days: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=90)
    expiry_forbid_days: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0)
    low_stock_alert: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=True)

    # 审计
    is_effective: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=True)
    effective_from: Mapped[date] = mapped_column(Date, nullable=False,
                                                  server_default=func.current_date())
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=1)
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False,
                                                 server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False,
        server_default=func.current_timestamp(), onupdate=func.current_timestamp(),
    )
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m10_stock_policy_key", "store_id", "product_code", "sku_key", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE AND is_effective = TRUE")),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_policy_lookup", "store_id", "sku_key", "policy_level"),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_policy_kind", "store_id", "product_kind", "policy_level"),
        Index("idx_m10_stock_policy_reorder", "store_id", "qty_reorder_point",
              postgresql_where=text("low_stock_alert = TRUE")),
        CheckConstraint("policy_level IN ('KIND','PRODUCT','SKU')",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_policy_level"),
        CheckConstraint("product_kind IN ('FRAME','LENS','CONTACT','ORTHO_K','RGP',"
                        "'CARE','SUN','ACCESSORY','SERVICE')",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_policy_kind"),
        CheckConstraint("(policy_level = 'KIND' AND product_code = '*' AND sku_key = '*') "
                        "OR (policy_level = 'PRODUCT' AND product_code <> '*' "
                        "AND sku_key = '*') OR (policy_level = 'SKU' "
                        "AND product_code <> '*' AND sku_key <> '*')",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_policy_granularity"),
        CheckConstraint("qty_safety >= 0 AND qty_reorder_point >= 0 "
                        "AND qty_min_display >= 0 "
                        "AND (qty_max IS NULL OR qty_max >= qty_reorder_point)",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_policy_qty"),
        CheckConstraint("qty_reorder_point >= qty_safety",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_policy_reorder_ge_safety"),
        CheckConstraint("reserve_ttl_minutes > 0 "
                        "AND reserve_ttl_minutes <= reserve_ttl_urgent_minutes "
                        "AND reserve_ttl_urgent_minutes <= reserve_ttl_custom_minutes "
                        "AND reserve_ttl_custom_minutes <= 10080",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_policy_ttl"),
        CheckConstraint("(allow_oversell = FALSE AND oversell_limit = 0) "
                        "OR (allow_oversell = TRUE AND oversell_limit >= 0)",
                        name="ck_m10_stock_policy_oversell"),
        CheckConstraint("reconcile_tolerance >= 0 AND approval_loss_amount >= 0 "
                        "AND expiry_alert_days >= 0 AND expiry_forbid_days >= 0 "
                        "AND check_cycle_days > 0", name="ck_m10_stock_policy_tolerance"),
    )


# ==================================================================
# 加工域：m10_lab_*
# ==================================================================


class M10LabJob(Base):
    """加工单主表：一副眼镜从付款到可交付的全过程。

    状态机 8 态：PENDING → ASSIGNED → PROCESSING → (OUTSOURCED) →
    QC_PENDING → QC_FAILED → …… → COMPLETED，含外协分支与质检返工分支。
    返工时新建 job_type='REWORK' 的新单，parent_job_id 指回原单，
    原单不被修改，保证每轮加工的执行历史与工时独立可考。
    """

    __tablename__ = "m10_lab_job"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    lab_job_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    store_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    store_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)

    # 来源单据
    order_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    order_item_seq: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=1)
    sales_order_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    rx_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    customer_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    patient_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    customer_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    customer_phone: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))

    # 商品
    product_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    product_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), nullable=False)
    product_kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)
    frame_product_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    frame_product_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(200))
    frame_shape: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))
    supply_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="STOCK")

    # 处方参数
    eye_side: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=False, default="OU")
    sph_od: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    cyl_od: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    axis_od: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    sph_os: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    cyl_os: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    axis_os: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    pd_total: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    pd_od: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    pd_os: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    pd_height_od: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    pd_height_os: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    lens_design: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    add_power: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    prism_value: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    prism_base: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(8))

    # 工序模板（来自 M01-08 API-50）
    template_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    template_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(128))
    template_matched_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    template_match_source: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))
    step_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0)
    total_minutes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    actual_minutes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)

    # 状态
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="PENDING")
    current_step_seq: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    current_step_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    progress_percent: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0)

    # 优先级与交期
    priority: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=5)
    is_urgent: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    is_overdue: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    promised_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False)
    planned_start_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    planned_finish_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    started_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    finished_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    ready_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    lead_time_days: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0)
    expected_piece_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    piece_arrived_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)

    # 外协与返工
    has_outsource: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    outsource_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0)
    rework_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=1)
    parent_job_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    root_job_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    job_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="NORMAL")

    # 质检结果快照
    qc_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="PENDING")
    qc_fail_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(256))
    defect_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))

    # 交付与通知
    notify_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False,
                                               default="NOT_SENT")
    notify_channel: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))
    notified_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    delivered_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)

    # 审计
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1024))
    cancel_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    cancelled_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    cancelled_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=1)
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False,
                                                 server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False,
        server_default=func.current_timestamp(), onupdate=func.current_timestamp(),
    )
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m10_lab_job_no", "lab_job_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("uk_m10_lab_job_order_item", "order_no", "order_item_seq", "rework_count",
              unique=True, postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_job_board", "store_id", "priority", "promised_at",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "status IN ('PENDING','ASSIGNED','PROCESSING','OUTSOURCED',"
                  "'QC_PENDING','QC_FAILED') AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_job_status", "status", "promised_at"),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_job_order", "order_no"),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_job_customer", "customer_id", text("created_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_job_patient", "patient_id", text("created_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_job_promised", "promised_at",
              postgresql_where=text("status NOT IN ('COMPLETED','CANCELLED')")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_job_outsource", "has_outsource", "status",
              postgresql_where=text("has_outsource = TRUE")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_job_parent", "parent_job_id",
              postgresql_where=text("parent_job_id IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_job_ready", "ready_at", "store_id",
              postgresql_where=text("status = 'COMPLETED' AND delivered_at IS NULL")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_job_notify", "notify_status",
              postgresql_where=text("notify_status IN ('NOT_SENT','FAILED') "
                                    "AND status = 'COMPLETED'")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_job_name_trgm", "customer_name",
              postgresql_using="gin",
              postgresql_ops={"customer_name": "gin_trgm_ops"}),
        CheckConstraint("lab_job_no ~ '^JG[0-9]{12}$'", name="ck_m10_lab_job_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint("status IN ('PENDING','ASSIGNED','PROCESSING','OUTSOURCED',"
                        "'QC_PENDING','QC_FAILED','COMPLETED','CANCELLED')",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_job_status"),
        CheckConstraint("job_type IN ('NORMAL','REWORK','REPAIR','REMAKE')",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_job_type"),
        CheckConstraint("qc_status IN ('PENDING','PASSED','FAILED','PARTIAL')",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_job_qc"),
        CheckConstraint("defect_type IS NULL OR defect_type IN ('POWER_ERROR',"
                        "'AXIS_ERROR','MOUNT_OFFSET','SCRATCH','POWER_MISMATCH',"
                        "'FRAME_ASYMMETRY')", name="ck_m10_lab_job_defect"),
        CheckConstraint("notify_status IN ('NOT_SENT','SENT','FAILED','NOT_REQUIRED')",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_job_notify"),
        CheckConstraint("notify_channel IS NULL OR notify_channel IN "
                        "('SMS','WECHAT','PHONE','APP')", name="ck_m10_lab_job_channel"),
        CheckConstraint("supply_type IN ('STOCK','CUSTOM')",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_job_supply"),
        CheckConstraint("supply_type <> 'CUSTOM' OR (lead_time_days > 0 "
                        "AND expected_piece_at IS NOT NULL)", name="ck_m10_lab_job_lead"),
        CheckConstraint("eye_side IN ('OD','OS','OU','NA')", name="ck_m10_lab_job_eye"),
        CheckConstraint("(sph_od IS NULL OR (sph_od * 4) = trunc(sph_od * 4)) "
                        "AND (sph_os IS NULL OR (sph_os * 4) = trunc(sph_os * 4))",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_job_sph_step"),
        CheckConstraint("(cyl_od IS NULL OR (cyl_od * 4) = trunc(cyl_od * 4)) "
                        "AND (cyl_os IS NULL OR (cyl_os * 4) = trunc(cyl_os * 4))",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_job_cyl_step"),
        CheckConstraint("((axis_od IS NULL AND (cyl_od IS NULL OR cyl_od = 0)) "
                        "OR (axis_od BETWEEN 1 AND 180 AND cyl_od <> 0)) "
                        "AND ((axis_os IS NULL AND (cyl_os IS NULL OR cyl_os = 0)) "
                        "OR (axis_os BETWEEN 1 AND 180 AND cyl_os <> 0))",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_job_axis"),
        CheckConstraint("lens_design NOT IN ('PROGRESSIVE','BIFOCAL') "
                        "OR (pd_height_od IS NOT NULL AND pd_height_os IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND add_power IS NOT NULL AND add_power > 0)",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_job_progressive"),
        CheckConstraint("priority BETWEEN 1 AND 9", name="ck_m10_lab_job_priority"),
        CheckConstraint("progress_percent BETWEEN 0 AND 100",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_job_progress"),
        CheckConstraint("promised_at >= created_at + INTERVAL '30 minutes'",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_job_promised"),
        CheckConstraint("(started_at IS NULL OR started_at >= created_at) "
                        "AND (finished_at IS NULL OR started_at IS NULL "
                        "OR finished_at >= started_at) AND (ready_at IS NULL "
                        "OR finished_at IS NULL OR ready_at >= finished_at)",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_job_time_order"),
        CheckConstraint("status <> 'COMPLETED' OR (finished_at IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND ready_at IS NOT NULL AND qc_status = 'PASSED' "
                        "AND notify_status IN ('SENT','NOT_REQUIRED'))",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_job_completed"),
        CheckConstraint("status <> 'QC_FAILED' OR (qc_status = 'FAILED' "
                        "AND defect_type IS NOT NULL AND qc_fail_reason IS NOT NULL)",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_job_qc_failed"),
        CheckConstraint("(status <> 'OUTSOURCED' AND has_outsource = FALSE) "
                        "OR outsource_count >= 1", name="ck_m10_lab_job_outsourcing"),
        CheckConstraint("job_type = 'NORMAL' OR (parent_job_id IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND root_job_no IS NOT NULL)", name="ck_m10_lab_job_rework"),
        CheckConstraint("rework_count >= 1 AND step_count >= 0 AND total_minutes >= 0 "
                        "AND actual_minutes >= 0 AND outsource_count >= 0",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_job_rework_count"),
        CheckConstraint("status <> 'CANCELLED' OR (cancel_reason IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND cancelled_by IS NOT NULL AND cancelled_at IS NOT NULL)",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_job_cancelled"),
        CheckConstraint("version >= 1", name="ck_m10_lab_job_version"),
    )


class M10LabJobStep(Base):
    """加工工序执行表：记录每道工序的开始/完成/质检事实。

    工序定义（process_type / standard_minutes / process_fee / is_outsourced /
    qc_items）来自 M01-08 md_optical_process，本表只记录执行事实。
    """

    __tablename__ = "m10_lab_job_step"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    lab_job_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    lab_job_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    step_seq: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False)

    # 工序定义快照（只读引用 M01-08）
    process_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    process_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    process_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)
    is_optional: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    is_skipped: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    is_outsourced: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    outsourced_supplier_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    standard_minutes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    process_fee: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(10, 2), nullable=False,
                                                 default=Decimal("0"))
    qc_items: Mapped[Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)

    # 派工与执行
    assigned_operator_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    assigned_operator_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="PENDING")
    started_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    finished_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    actual_minutes: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer)
    operator_remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))

    # 工序质检
    qc_required: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    qc_result: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))
    qc_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    qc_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)

    # 外协关联
    outsource_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    skip_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(256))

    # 审计
```
