```python
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=1)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False,
                                                 server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False,
        server_default=func.current_timestamp(), onupdate=func.current_timestamp(),
    )
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m10_lab_job_step_seq", "lab_job_id", "step_seq", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_job_step_job", "lab_job_id", "step_seq"),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_job_step_current", "lab_job_no", "status",
              postgresql_where=text("status IN ('PENDING','READY','PROCESSING',"
                                    "'OUTSOURCED')")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_job_step_operator", "assigned_operator_id", "status",
              "started_at", postgresql_where=text("status IN ('READY','PROCESSING')")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_job_step_type_stat", "process_type", "finished_at"),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_job_step_outsource", "outsource_id",
              postgresql_where=text("outsource_id IS NOT NULL")),
        CheckConstraint("step_seq >= 1", name="ck_m10_lab_job_step_seq"),
        CheckConstraint("process_type IN ('EDGING','CUTTING','MOUNTING','GROOVING',"
                        "'DRILLING','POLISHING','TINTING','COATING','REINFORCE',"
                        "'ADJUSTING','CLEANING')", name="ck_m10_lab_job_step_type"),
        CheckConstraint("status IN ('PENDING','READY','PROCESSING','OUTSOURCED',"
                        "'COMPLETED','QC_PASSED','QC_FAILED','SKIPPED')",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_job_step_status"),
        CheckConstraint("qc_result IS NULL OR qc_result IN ('PASS','FAIL')",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_job_step_qc"),
        CheckConstraint("standard_minutes >= 0 AND (actual_minutes IS NULL "
                        "OR actual_minutes >= 0) AND process_fee >= 0",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_job_step_minutes"),
        CheckConstraint("status NOT IN ('COMPLETED','QC_PASSED','QC_FAILED') "
                        "OR (started_at IS NOT NULL AND finished_at IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND finished_at >= started_at AND actual_minutes IS NOT NULL)",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_job_step_completed"),
        CheckConstraint("status <> 'PROCESSING' OR started_at IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_job_step_processing"),
        CheckConstraint("(is_skipped = FALSE AND status <> 'SKIPPED') "
                        "OR (is_skipped = TRUE AND status = 'SKIPPED' "
                        "AND skip_reason IS NOT NULL)", name="ck_m10_lab_job_step_skip"),
        CheckConstraint("is_outsourced = FALSE OR (outsourced_supplier_code IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND (status <> 'OUTSOURCED' OR outsource_id IS NOT NULL))",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_job_step_outsourced"),
        CheckConstraint("qc_required = FALSE OR status NOT IN ('QC_PASSED','QC_FAILED') "
                        "OR (qc_result IS NOT NULL AND qc_by IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND qc_at IS NOT NULL)", name="ck_m10_lab_job_step_qc_required"),
    )


class M10LabAssign(Base):
    """加工派工表：每道工序派给谁、何时派、改派历史。

    status='ACTIVE' 的记录数即加工师实时负载，是派工排序算法的输入。
    """

    __tablename__ = "m10_lab_assign"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    assign_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    lab_job_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    lab_job_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    step_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    step_seq: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    store_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)

    # 加工师
    operator_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    operator_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    operator_group: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))

    # 派工方式
    assign_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="MANUAL")
    assign_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    priority_at_assign: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=5)
    queue_position: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer)

    # 负载快照
    operator_pending_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False,
                                                        default=0)
    operator_load_minutes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    operator_capacity_minutes: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer)

    # 状态
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="ACTIVE")
    assigned_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False,
                                                  server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    accepted_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    completed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    reassigned_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    reassign_from_operator_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    reassign_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(256))

    # 审计
    assigned_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    assigned_by_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False,
                                                 server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False,
        server_default=func.current_timestamp(), onupdate=func.current_timestamp(),
    )
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m10_lab_assign_no", "assign_no", unique=True),
        Index("uk_m10_lab_assign_active_step", "step_id", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("status = 'ACTIVE' AND step_id IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("uk_m10_lab_assign_active_job", "lab_job_id", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("status = 'ACTIVE' AND step_id IS NULL")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_assign_operator", "operator_id", "assigned_at",
              postgresql_where=text("status = 'ACTIVE'")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_assign_job", "lab_job_id", text("assigned_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_assign_operator_day", "operator_id", "assigned_at"),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_assign_status", "status", "store_id"),
        CheckConstraint("assign_no ~ '^PG[0-9]{13}$'", name="ck_m10_lab_assign_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint("assign_type IN ('MANUAL','AUTO','GRAB','REASSIGN','SYSTEM')",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_assign_type"),
        CheckConstraint("status IN ('ACTIVE','COMPLETED','REASSIGNED','CANCELLED')",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_assign_status"),
        CheckConstraint("assign_reason IS NULL OR assign_reason IN ('URGENT','OVERDUE',"
                        "'SKILL_MATCH','LOAD_BALANCE','OUTSOURCE_RETURN',"
                        "'MANUAL_OVERRIDE')", name="ck_m10_lab_assign_reason"),
        CheckConstraint("priority_at_assign BETWEEN 1 AND 9 AND operator_pending_count >= 0 "
                        "AND operator_load_minutes >= 0 "
                        "AND (operator_capacity_minutes IS NULL "
                        "OR operator_capacity_minutes > 0)",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_assign_numeric"),
        CheckConstraint("(step_id IS NULL AND step_seq IS NULL) "
                        "OR (step_id IS NOT NULL AND step_seq IS NOT NULL)",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_assign_step_pair"),
        CheckConstraint("status <> 'REASSIGNED' OR (reassign_reason IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND reassigned_at IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND reassign_from_operator_id IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND reassign_from_operator_id <> operator_id)",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_assign_reassign"),
        CheckConstraint("status <> 'COMPLETED' OR (completed_at IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND completed_at >= assigned_at)",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_assign_completed"),
        CheckConstraint("accepted_at IS NULL OR accepted_at >= assigned_at",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_assign_accepted"),
    )


class M10LabOutsource(Base):
    """外协加工单表：外送、承诺回片、实际回片、验收、费用。"""

    __tablename__ = "m10_lab_outsource"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    outsource_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    lab_job_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    lab_job_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    step_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    step_seq: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False)
    process_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    process_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)
    store_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)

    # 供应商
    supplier_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    supplier_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    supplier_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), nullable=False)
    supplier_contact: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    supplier_phone: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))

    # 外送与回片
    qty: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False, default=Decimal("1"))
    sent_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False)
    expected_return_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False)
    standard_return_days: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=3)
    returned_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    is_overdue: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    overdue_days: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0)

    # 验收
    send_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    send_by_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    received_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    received_by_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    inspect_result: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))
    inspect_remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    defect_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    return_times: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0)

    # 费用
    outsource_fee: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(10, 2), nullable=False,
                                                   default=Decimal("0"))
    fee_settle_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False,
                                                   default="UNSETTLED")
    settle_ref_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))

    # 状态
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="SENT")
    cancel_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(256))
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512))
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=1)
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False,
                                                 server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False,
        server_default=func.current_timestamp(), onupdate=func.current_timestamp(),
    )
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m10_lab_outsource_no", "outsource_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_outsource_job", "lab_job_id", "step_seq"),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_outsource_open", "expected_return_at", "store_id",
              postgresql_where=text("status IN ('SENT','RECEIVED')")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_outsource_supplier", "supplier_code", text("sent_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_outsource_settle", "supplier_code", "outsource_fee",
              postgresql_where=text("fee_settle_status = 'UNSETTLED'")),
        CheckConstraint("outsource_no ~ '^WX[0-9]{12}$'",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_outsource_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint("status IN ('DRAFT','SENT','RECEIVED','INSPECTED',"
                        "'REJECTED','CLOSED','CANCELLED')",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_outsource_status"),
        CheckConstraint("inspect_result IS NULL OR inspect_result IN "
                        "('PASS','FAIL','REWORK','RETURN_TO_SUPPLIER')",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_outsource_inspect"),
        CheckConstraint("defect_type IS NULL OR defect_type IN "
                        "('SCRATCH','COATING_DEFECT','POWER_ERROR','OTHER')",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_outsource_defect"),
        CheckConstraint("fee_settle_status IN ('UNSETTLED','SETTLED','VOID')",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_outsource_settle"),
        CheckConstraint("qty > 0 AND outsource_fee >= 0 AND standard_return_days > 0 "
                        "AND return_times >= 0 AND overdue_days >= 0",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_outsource_amount"),
        CheckConstraint("expected_return_at > sent_at",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_outsource_return_order"),
        CheckConstraint("status NOT IN ('RECEIVED','INSPECTED','REJECTED','CLOSED') "
                        "OR returned_at IS NOT NULL", name="ck_m10_lab_outsource_returned"),
        CheckConstraint("status NOT IN ('INSPECTED','REJECTED') "
                        "OR (inspect_result IS NOT NULL AND received_by IS NOT NULL)",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_outsource_inspected"),
        CheckConstraint("inspect_result IS NULL OR inspect_result = 'PASS' "
                        "OR (inspect_remark IS NOT NULL AND defect_type IS NOT NULL)",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_outsource_reject_reason"),
        CheckConstraint("fee_settle_status <> 'SETTLED' OR settle_ref_no IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_outsource_settled"),
        CheckConstraint("status <> 'CANCELLED' OR cancel_reason IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_outsource_cancelled"),
        CheckConstraint("(is_overdue = FALSE AND overdue_days = 0) "
                        "OR (is_overdue = TRUE AND overdue_days > 0)",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_outsource_overdue"),
    )


class M10LabQc(Base):
    """加工质检单表：整单终检与外协验收，含质检项 JSONB、实测值、缺陷分类与返工判定。"""

    __tablename__ = "m10_lab_qc"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    qc_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    lab_job_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    lab_job_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    store_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    qc_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="FINAL")
    qc_round: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=1)

    # 判定
    qc_result: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)
    dispose_decision: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))
    fail_item_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0)
    fail_items: Mapped[Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    defect_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    defect_desc: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1024))
    qc_items: Mapped[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=False,
                                                           default=list)

    # 实测值
    measured_sph_od: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    measured_sph_os: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    measured_cyl_od: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    measured_cyl_os: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2))
    measured_axis_od: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    measured_axis_os: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    measured_pd: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    measured_pd_od: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    measured_pd_os: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    power_deviation_od: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    power_deviation_os: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))
    axis_deviation_od: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    axis_deviation_os: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    pd_deviation: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2))

    # 返工关联
    rework_job_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    rework_reason_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    rework_chargeable: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    rework_affects_promise: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False,
                                                         default=False)
    new_promised_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    concession_approval_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))

    # 执行
    qc_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    qc_by_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    qc_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False,
                                            server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    duration_seconds: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer)

    # 审计
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1024))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False,
                                                 server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False,
        server_default=func.current_timestamp(), onupdate=func.current_timestamp(),
    )
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m10_lab_qc_no", "qc_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("uk_m10_lab_qc_round", "lab_job_id", "qc_round", "qc_type", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_qc_job", "lab_job_id", text("qc_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_qc_fail", "defect_type", text("qc_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("qc_result = 'FAIL'")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_qc_rework", "rework_job_id",
              postgresql_where=text("rework_job_id IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_qc_concession", "qc_at",
              postgresql_where=text("dispose_decision = 'CONCESSION'")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_qc_qcby", "qc_by", text("qc_at DESC")),
        CheckConstraint("qc_no ~ '^ZJ[0-9]{13}$'", name="ck_m10_lab_qc_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint("qc_type IN ('FINAL','OUTSOURCE','REWORK','SPOT')",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_qc_type"),
        CheckConstraint("qc_result IN ('PASS','FAIL')", name="ck_m10_lab_qc_result"),
        CheckConstraint("dispose_decision IS NULL OR dispose_decision IN ('RELEASE',"
                        "'REWORK','CONCESSION','SCRAP','RETURN_TO_SUPPLIER')",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_qc_decision"),
        CheckConstraint("defect_type IS NULL OR defect_type IN ('POWER_ERROR',"
                        "'AXIS_ERROR','MOUNT_OFFSET','SCRATCH','POWER_MISMATCH',"
                        "'FRAME_ASYMMETRY')", name="ck_m10_lab_qc_defect"),
        CheckConstraint("qc_round >= 1 AND fail_item_count >= 0 "
                        "AND (duration_seconds IS NULL OR duration_seconds >= 0)",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_qc_numeric"),
        CheckConstraint("jsonb_typeof(qc_items) = 'array'", name="ck_m10_lab_qc_items_array"),
        CheckConstraint("fail_items IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(fail_items) = 'array'",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_qc_fail_items"),
        CheckConstraint("qc_result <> 'FAIL' OR (defect_type IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND defect_desc IS NOT NULL AND fail_item_count > 0 "
                        "AND fail_items IS NOT NULL)", name="ck_m10_lab_qc_failed"),
        CheckConstraint("qc_result <> 'PASS' OR fail_item_count = 0",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_qc_passed"),
        CheckConstraint("(qc_result = 'PASS' AND (dispose_decision IS NULL "
                        "OR dispose_decision = 'RELEASE')) OR (qc_result = 'FAIL' "
                        "AND dispose_decision IN ('REWORK','CONCESSION','SCRAP',"
                        "'RETURN_TO_SUPPLIER'))",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_qc_decision_consistency"),
        CheckConstraint("dispose_decision <> 'REWORK' OR (rework_job_id IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND rework_reason_code IS NOT NULL)", name="ck_m10_lab_qc_rework"),
        CheckConstraint("rework_affects_promise = FALSE OR new_promised_at IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_qc_promise"),
        CheckConstraint("dispose_decision <> 'CONCESSION' "
                        "OR concession_approval_no IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_qc_concession"),
        CheckConstraint("qc_by IS NOT NULL AND qc_by_name IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_qc_by"),
    )


class M10LabStat(Base):
    """加工与库存日统计表：按 日期×门店×加工师 / 日期×门店×品类 / 日期×门店 三维度预聚合。"""

    __tablename__ = "m10_lab_stat"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    stat_date: Mapped[date] = mapped_column(Date, nullable=False)
    store_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    store_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    stat_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="OPERATOR")
    operator_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    operator_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    product_kind: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))

    # 加工量
    job_created_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    job_completed_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    job_cancelled_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    job_rework_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    step_completed_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    job_overdue_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    job_outsource_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)

    # 工时
    original_minutes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    rework_minutes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    outsource_minutes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    standard_minutes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    idle_minutes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    capacity_minutes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    overtime_minutes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)

    # 质量
    qc_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    qc_pass_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    qc_fail_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    concession_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    scrap_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    on_time_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)

    # 库存
    sku_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    batch_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    qty_on_hand: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(14, 4), nullable=False,
                                                 default=Decimal("0"))
    qty_reserved: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(14, 4), nullable=False,
                                                  default=Decimal("0"))
    qty_below_safety: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    stock_amount: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(14, 2), nullable=False,
                                                  default=Decimal("0"))
    in_qty: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(14, 4), nullable=False,
                                            default=Decimal("0"))
    out_qty: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(14, 4), nullable=False,
                                             default=Decimal("0"))
    turnover_rate: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(8, 4), nullable=False,
                                                   default=Decimal("0"))
    expiry_alert_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    reconcile_diff_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)

    # 生成列比率
    pass_rate: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(6, 4),
        computed=text("CASE WHEN qc_count > 0 "
                      "THEN ROUND(qc_pass_count::NUMERIC / qc_count, 4) ELSE 0 END"),
        nullable=False,
    )
    on_time_rate: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(6, 4),
        computed=text("CASE WHEN job_completed_count > 0 "
                      "THEN ROUND(on_time_count::NUMERIC / job_completed_count, 4) "
                      "ELSE 0 END"),
        nullable=False,
    )
    capacity_utilization: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(6, 4),
        computed=text("CASE WHEN capacity_minutes > 0 "
                      "THEN ROUND((original_minutes + rework_minutes)::NUMERIC "
                      "/ capacity_minutes, 4) ELSE 0 END"),
        nullable=False,
    )

    # 审计
    calculated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False,
                                                    server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    calc_version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=1)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False,
                                                 server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False,
        server_default=func.current_timestamp(), onupdate=func.current_timestamp(),
    )

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m10_lab_stat_key", "stat_date", "store_id", "stat_type",
              "operator_id", "product_kind", unique=True),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_stat_date", text("stat_date DESC"), "store_id"),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_stat_operator", "operator_id", text("stat_date DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("stat_type = 'OPERATOR'")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_stat_kind", "product_kind", text("stat_date DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("stat_type = 'KIND'")),
        Index("idx_m10_lab_stat_store", "store_id", text("stat_date DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("stat_type = 'STORE'")),
        CheckConstraint("stat_type IN ('OPERATOR','KIND','STORE')",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_stat_type"),
        CheckConstraint("(stat_type = 'OPERATOR' AND operator_id IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND product_kind IS NULL) OR (stat_type = 'KIND' "
                        "AND product_kind IS NOT NULL AND operator_id IS NULL) "
                        "OR (stat_type = 'STORE' AND operator_id IS NULL "
                        "AND product_kind IS NULL)", name="ck_m10_lab_stat_dims"),
        CheckConstraint("product_kind IS NULL OR product_kind IN ('FRAME','LENS',"
                        "'CONTACT','ORTHO_K','RGP','CARE','SUN','ACCESSORY','SERVICE')",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_stat_kind"),
        CheckConstraint("job_created_count >= 0 AND job_completed_count >= 0 "
                        "AND job_cancelled_count >= 0 AND job_rework_count >= 0 "
                        "AND step_completed_count >= 0 AND job_overdue_count >= 0 "
                        "AND job_outsource_count >= 0 AND qc_count >= 0 "
                        "AND qc_pass_count >= 0 AND qc_fail_count >= 0 "
                        "AND concession_count >= 0 AND scrap_count >= 0 "
                        "AND on_time_count >= 0 AND sku_count >= 0 "
                        "AND batch_count >= 0 AND qty_below_safety >= 0 "
                        "AND expiry_alert_count >= 0 AND reconcile_diff_count >= 0",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_stat_counts"),
        CheckConstraint("original_minutes >= 0 AND rework_minutes >= 0 "
                        "AND outsource_minutes >= 0 AND standard_minutes >= 0 "
                        "AND idle_minutes >= 0 AND capacity_minutes >= 0 "
                        "AND overtime_minutes >= 0", name="ck_m10_lab_stat_minutes"),
        CheckConstraint("qty_on_hand >= 0 AND qty_reserved >= 0 AND stock_amount >= 0 "
                        "AND in_qty >= 0 AND out_qty >= 0 AND turnover_rate >= 0",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_stat_qty"),
        CheckConstraint("qc_pass_count + qc_fail_count <= qc_count "
                        "AND on_time_count <= job_completed_count",
                        name="ck_m10_lab_stat_ratio_bound"),
        CheckConstraint("calc_version >= 1", name="ck_m10_lab_stat_calc"),
    )
```

### 4.15 Pydantic Schema 定义

> 命名约定：`XxxCreateIn` / `XxxUpdateIn` / `XxxQuery` 为请求体，`XxxOut` / `XxxListOut` / `XxxBrief` 为响应体。全部使用 Pydantic V2，校验逻辑用 `field_validator` / `model_validator(mode="after")`。

```python
"""M10-07 视光库存与加工作业 —— Pydantic V2 Schema"""

from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Literal, Optional

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator, model_validator


# ==================== 枚举字面量 ====================

StockBatchStatus = Literal["AVAILABLE", "LOCKED", "DEPLETED", "EXPIRED",
                           "DAMAGED", "FROZEN"]
StockReserveStatus = Literal["ACTIVE", "CONSUMED", "RELEASED", "EXPIRED", "CANCELLED"]
StockTxnType = Literal["IN_ISSUE", "IN_RETURN", "IN_CHECK", "OUT_SALE",
                       "OUT_LAB", "OUT_CHECK", "OUT_SCRAP", "ADJUST_REVERSE"]
StockCheckStatus = Literal["DRAFT", "CHECKING", "SUBMITTED", "CONFIRMED",
                           "REJECTED", "CANCELLED"]
LabJobStatus = Literal["PENDING", "ASSIGNED", "PROCESSING", "OUTSOURCED",
                       "QC_PENDING", "QC_FAILED", "COMPLETED", "CANCELLED"]
LabJobType = Literal["NORMAL", "REWORK", "REPAIR", "REMAKE"]
LabStepStatus = Literal["PENDING", "READY", "PROCESSING", "OUTSOURCED",
                        "COMPLETED", "QC_PASSED", "QC_FAILED", "SKIPPED"]
ProcessType = Literal["EDGING", "CUTTING", "MOUNTING", "GROOVING", "DRILLING",
                      "POLISHING", "TINTING", "COATING", "REINFORCE",
                      "ADJUSTING", "CLEANING"]
DefectType = Literal["POWER_ERROR", "AXIS_ERROR", "MOUNT_OFFSET", "SCRATCH",
                     "POWER_MISMATCH", "FRAME_ASYMMETRY"]
EyeSide = Literal["OD", "OS", "OU", "NA"]
SupplyType = Literal["STOCK", "CUSTOM"]
NotifyChannel = Literal["SMS", "WECHAT", "PHONE", "APP"]


# ==================== 库存：请求体 ====================

class StockReserveItemIn(BaseModel):
    """单条预占请求行（P-19 内层结构）。"""

    sku_key: str = Field(..., max_length=96, description="SKU 键，格式 {product_code}｜{sph}｜{cyl}｜{axis}")
    product_code: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="产品编码（M01-08）")
    qty: Decimal = Field(Decimal("1"), gt=0, le=99, description="预占数量，必须大于 0")
    source_item_seq: Optional[int] = Field(None, ge=1, description="来源单据行号")
    batch_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="指定批次 ID（不填则按 FEFO 自动选批）")


class StockReserveIn(BaseModel):
    """库存预占请求体（P-19 `POST /internal/v1/m10/stock/reserve`）。"""

    store_id: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="门店 ID")
    source_type: Literal["SALES_ORDER", "LAB_JOB", "MANUAL", "REWORK"] = Field(
        "SALES_ORDER", description="来源类型")
    source_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="来源单号（订单号/加工单号）")
    source_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="来源单据主键 ID")
    sales_order_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="M16-04 销售单号")
    is_urgent: bool = Field(False, description="是否加急（影响预占 TTL：加急 72h，普通 24h）")
    items: List[StockReserveItemIn] = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=20,
                                            description="预占明细，一次最多 20 行")
    operator_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="操作员 ID")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="备注")

    @field_validator("items")
    @classmethod
    def _no_duplicate_sku(cls, v: List[StockReserveItemIn]) -> List[StockReserveItemIn]:
        """同一请求内不允许同一 SKU 出现两次（会绕过部分唯一索引）。"""
        keys = [i.sku_key for i in v]
        if len(keys) != len(set(keys)):
            raise ValueError("同一预占请求内 sku_key 不可重复")
        return v


class StockReleaseIn(BaseModel):
    """库存释放请求体（P-20 `POST /internal/v1/m10/stock/release`）。"""

    source_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="来源单号")
    source_type: Optional[Literal["SALES_ORDER", "LAB_JOB", "MANUAL", "REWORK"]] = Field(
        None, description="来源类型（不填则按单号全匹配）")
    reserve_nos: Optional[List[str]] = Field(None, max_length=50,
                                             description="指定释放的预占号，不填则释放该单全部有效预占")
    release_reason: Literal["ORDER_CANCELLED", "TTL_EXPIRED", "SHORTAGE",
                            "MANUAL", "REFUNDED"] = Field(
        "ORDER_CANCELLED", description="释放原因")
    operator_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="操作员 ID")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="备注")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _partial_release_needs_nos(self) -> "StockReleaseIn":
        """部分释放必须显式列出预占号，避免误释放整单。"""
        if self.release_reason == "MANUAL" and not self.reserve_nos:
            raise ValueError("人工释放必须指定 reserve_nos")
        return self


class StockAvailableQuery(BaseModel):
    """可销售量查询参数（P-18 `GET /internal/v1/m10/stock/available`）。"""

    store_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="门店 ID；不填则返回全部门店汇总")
    sku_keys: List[str] = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=100,
                                description="SKU 键列表，最多 100 个")
    include_custom: bool = Field(False, description="是否含定制（CUSTOM）的可供判断")
    biz_type: Literal["RETAIL", "LAB"] = Field(
        "RETAIL", description="业务场景 RETAIL 门市销售 / LAB 加工领用")

    @field_validator("sku_keys")
    @classmethod
    def _cap_and_dedup(cls, v: List[str]) -> List[str]:
        """去重并保持顺序，避免重复计算。"""
        seen, out = set(), []
        for k in v:
            if k not in seen:
                seen.add(k)
                out.append(k)
        return out


class StockCheckCreateIn(BaseModel):
    """新建盘点单请求体。"""

    store_id: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="门店 ID")
    check_type: Literal["MONTHLY", "SPOT", "FULL", "SPECIAL"] = Field(
        "MONTHLY", description="盘点类型")
    check_scope: Literal["ALL", "KIND", "SKU", "BATCH"] = Field(
        "ALL", description="盘点范围")
    product_kinds: Optional[List[str]] = Field(None, max_length=9,
                                               description="范围=KIND 时的品类列表")
    sku_keys: Optional[List[str]] = Field(None, max_length=500,
                                          description="范围=SKU 时的 SKU 列表")
    check_date: date = Field(..., description="盘点日期")
    checker_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="盘点人 ID")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1024, description="备注")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _scope_consistency(self) -> "StockCheckCreateIn":
        """范围与过滤条件必须匹配。"""
        if self.check_scope == "KIND" and not self.product_kinds:
            raise ValueError("盘点范围为 KIND 时必须提供 product_kinds")
        if self.check_scope == "SKU" and not self.sku_keys:
            raise ValueError("盘点范围为 SKU 时必须提供 sku_keys")
        return self


class StockCheckEnterIn(BaseModel):
    """盘点结果录入请求体。"""

    check_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="盘点单号")
    lines: List[Dict[str, Any]] = Field(..., min_length=1,
                                        description="明细数组，元素含 batch_id/actual_qty/reason_code")
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=128, description="幂等键")


class StockScrapIn(BaseModel):
    """报废出库请求体。"""

    store_id: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="门店 ID")
    batch_id: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="批次 ID")
    qty: Decimal = Field(..., gt=0, description="报废数量")
    scrap_type: Literal["EXPIRED", "DAMAGED", "RECALLED", "OTHER"] = Field(
        ..., description="报废类型")
    reason: str = Field(..., min_length=5, max_length=512, description="报废原因（≥5 字）")
    approval_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64, description="审批单号（超 500 元必填）")


# ==================== 加工：请求体 ====================

class LabOnOrderPaidIn(BaseModel):
    """P-17 `POST /internal/v1/m10/lab/on-order-paid` 请求体。"""

    order_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="M10-06 配镜订单号")
    sales_order_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="M16-04 销售单号")
    paid_at: datetime = Field(..., description="收款完成时刻（ISO 8601 带时区）")
    paid_amount: Decimal = Field(..., ge=0, description="已收金额，单位元")
    is_urgent: bool = Field(False, description="是否加急（顾客付加急费）")
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]] = Field(
        ..., min_length=1, max_length=10,
        description="订单行数组，元素含 order_item_seq/product_code/frame_product_code/处方参数")
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=128,
                                           description="幂等键，不填则用 sales_order_no 派生")

    @field_validator("paid_at")
    @classmethod
    def _tz_aware(cls, v: datetime) -> datetime:
        """时间必须带时区，与共享规范 2.4 一致。"""
        if v.tzinfo is None:
            raise ValueError("paid_at 必须带时区（如 2026-10-31T09:15:22+08:00）")
        return v


class LabAssignIn(BaseModel):
    """派工请求体。"""

    lab_job_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="加工单号")
    step_seq: Optional[int] = Field(None, ge=1, description="工序序号；不填则为整单派工")
    operator_id: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="加工师 ID")
    assign_type: Literal["MANUAL", "AUTO", "GRAB", "SYSTEM"] = Field(
        "MANUAL", description="派工方式")
    assign_reason: Optional[Literal["URGENT", "OVERDUE", "SKILL_MATCH",
                                    "LOAD_BALANCE", "OUTSOURCE_RETURN",
                                    "MANUAL_OVERRIDE"]] = Field(
        None, description="派工原因")
    force: bool = Field(False, description="加工师产能不足时是否强制派工")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="备注")


class LabStepStartIn(BaseModel):
    """工序开工请求体。"""

    lab_job_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="加工单号")
    step_seq: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="工序序号")
    operator_id: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="加工师 ID")
    scan_code: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64, description="扫码内容（加工单条码）")


class LabStepCompleteIn(BaseModel):
    """工序完工请求体。"""

    lab_job_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="加工单号")
    step_seq: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="工序序号")
    operator_id: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="加工师 ID")
    actual_minutes: int = Field(..., ge=0, le=1440, description="实际工时，单位分钟")
    qc_result: Optional[Literal["PASS", "FAIL"]] = Field(
        None, description="工序自检结果（qc_required=true 时必填）")
    is_skipped: bool = Field(False, description="是否跳过（仅可选工序）")
    skip_reason: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=256, description="跳过原因")
    operator_remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="操作备注")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _skip_rule(self) -> "LabStepCompleteIn":
        """跳过必须有原因，且跳过的工序不计工时。"""
        if self.is_skipped and not self.skip_reason:
            raise ValueError("跳过工序必须填写 skip_reason")
        if self.is_skipped and self.actual_minutes != 0:
            raise ValueError("跳过的工序 actual_minutes 必须为 0")
        return self


class LabQcIn(BaseModel):
    """整单质检提交请求体。"""

    lab_job_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="加工单号")
    qc_type: Literal["FINAL", "OUTSOURCE", "REWORK", "SPOT"] = Field(
        "FINAL", description="质检类型")
    qc_items: List[Dict[str, Any]] = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=20,
                                           description="质检项与结果数组")
    measured: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict,
                                     description="实测值，键名同 measured_* 字段后缀")
    dispose_decision: Optional[Literal["RELEASE", "REWORK", "CONCESSION",
                                       "SCRAP", "RETURN_TO_SUPPLIER"]] = Field(
        None, description="处置决定（不合格时必填）")
    defect_type: Optional[DefectType] = Field(None, description="主缺陷类型（不合格必填）")
    defect_desc: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1024, description="缺陷描述")
    rework_affects_promise: bool = Field(False, description="返工是否顺延对客承诺")
    new_promised_at: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="顺延后的新承诺时刻")
    qc_by: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="质检员 ID")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _fail_rule(self) -> "LabQcIn":
        """不合格必须给出处置、缺陷类型与描述；返工顺延必须有新承诺时刻。"""
        failed = any(i.get("result") == "FAIL" for i in self.qc_items)
        if failed:
            if self.dispose_decision is None:
                raise ValueError("存在不合格项时必须给出 dispose_decision")
            if self.defect_type is None or not self.defect_desc:
                raise ValueError("不合格必须填写 defect_type 与 defect_desc")
        if self.rework_affects_promise and self.new_promised_at is None:
            raise ValueError("返工顺延交期必须提供 new_promised_at")
        return self


class LabOutsourceSendIn(BaseModel):
    """外送加工请求体。"""

    lab_job_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="加工单号")
    step_seq: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="外协工序序号")
    supplier_code: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="外协供应商编码")
    qty: Decimal = Field(Decimal("1"), gt=0, description="外送数量")
    standard_return_days: int = Field(3, ge=1, le=60, description="标准回片天数")
    fee: Decimal = Field(Decimal("0"), ge=0, description="外协加工费，单位元")
    operator_id: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="外送人 ID")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="备注")


class LabOutsourceReceiveIn(BaseModel):
    """外协回片登记请求体。"""

        ..., description="验收结果")
    defect_type: Optional[Literal["SCRATCH", "COATING_DEFECT", "POWER_ERROR",
                                  "OTHER"]] = Field(None, description="验收不合格时的缺陷类型")
    inspect_remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="验收说明")
    received_by: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="接收人 ID")
```

## 附录 A：表清单速查

| 序号 | 表名 | 中文名 | 主键 | 唯一键 | 分区 | 3 年量级 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `m10_stock_batch` | 门店二级库批次台账表 | `id` | `batch_key` | 无 | 8,000 行 |
| 2 | `m10_stock_reserve` | 库存预占表 | `id` | `reserve_no`、`idempotency_key`、(`sku_key`,`source_no`) 部分 | 无 | 87,000 行 |
| 3 | `m10_stock_movement` | 门店库出入库流水表 | `id`,`created_at` | `movement_no`、`idempotency_key` | 月 RANGE(`created_at`) | 80 万行 |
| 4 | `m10_stock_check` | 库存盘点单表 | `id` | `check_no` | 无 | 240 行 |
| 5 | `m10_stock_reconcile` | 库存对账差异表 | `id` | (`reconcile_date`,`reconcile_batch_no`,`warehouse_code`,`sku_key`,`reconcile_level`) | 无 | 9,000 行 |
| 6 | `m10_stock_policy` | 视光库存策略表 | `id` | (`store_id`,`product_code`,`sku_key`) 部分 | 无 | 2,000 行 |
| 7 | `m10_lab_job` | 加工单主表 | `id` | `lab_job_no`、(`order_no`,`order_item_seq`,`rework_count`) | 无 | 56,700 行 |
| 8 | `m10_lab_job_step` | 加工工序执行表 | `id` | (`lab_job_id`,`step_seq`) | 无 | 396,000 行 |
| 9 | `m10_lab_assign` | 加工派工表 | `id` | `assign_no`、`step_id` 部分、`lab_job_id` 部分 | 无 | 72,000 行 |
| 10 | `m10_lab_outsource` | 外协加工单表 | `id` | `outsource_no` | 无 | 10,800 行 |
| 11 | `m10_lab_qc` | 加工质检单表 | `id` | `qc_no`、(`lab_job_id`,`qc_round`,`qc_type`) | 无 | 59,400 行 |
| 12 | `m10_lab_stat` | 加工与库存统计表 | `id` | (`stat_date`,`store_id`,`stat_type`,`operator_id`,`product_kind`) | 无 | 61,000 行 |

**合计：** 12 张表；单年新增约 60 万行（其中 `m10_stock_movement` 40 万）；3 年数据总量约 1.06 GB（含索引）。

## 附录 B：状态枚举汇总

### B.1 门店批次状态（`m10_stock_batch.status`，6 态）

| 取值 | 中文 | 说明 | 终态 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `AVAILABLE` | 在架可售 | 可被预占与出库 | 否 |
| `LOCKED` | 锁定 | 盘点锁库期间禁止出库 | 否 |
| `DEPLETED` | 已售罄 | 在架量为 0，新品补货后可回 `AVAILABLE` | 否 |
| `EXPIRED` | 已过期 | 效期到期，只能报废 | 是 |
| `DAMAGED` | 破损/污染 | 报损登记，只能报废 | 是 |
| `FROZEN` | 冻结 | 产品停用/召回/审批冻结 | 否 |

### B.2 预占状态（`m10_stock_reserve.status`，5 态）

| 取值 | 中文 | 说明 | 是否占库存 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ACTIVE` | 有效 | 已承诺给某张单，尚未出库 | 是 |
| `CONSUMED` | 已消耗 | 已转实际出库（`OUT_SALE`） | 否 |
| `RELEASED` | 已释放 | 单据取消/退款，库存归还 | 否 |
| `EXPIRED` | 已过期 | TTL 到期自动释放 | 否 |
| `CANCELLED` | 已作废 | 人工作废 | 否 |

### B.3 库存事务类型（`m10_stock_movement.txn_type`，8 类）

| 取值 | 方向 | 中文 | 触发来源 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `IN_ISSUE` | IN | 门店领用入库 | P07 补货，M15-04 出库成功 |
| `IN_RETURN` | IN | 顾客退货入库 | 取镜交付退换 |
| `IN_CHECK` | IN | 盘盈 | 盘点确认 |
| `OUT_SALE` | OUT | 销售出库 | 预占转出库 |
| `OUT_LAB` | OUT | 加工领用出库 | 加工单派工 |
| `OUT_CHECK` | OUT | 盘亏 | 盘点确认 |
| `OUT_SCRAP` | OUT | 报废出库 | 报损审批完成 |
| `ADJUST_REVERSE` | IN/OUT | 冲正 | 异常校正审批 |

### B.4 盘点单状态（`m10_stock_check.status`，6 态）

| 取值 | 中文 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| `DRAFT` | 建单 | 尚未开始录入 |
| `CHECKING` | 录入中 | 盘点人正在录实盘数 |
| `SUBMITTED` | 待确认 | 已提交待店长确认 |
| `CONFIRMED` | 已确认 | 差异生效并已回传 M15 |
| `REJECTED` | 已驳回 | 店长驳回，需重新录入 |
| `CANCELLED` | 已作废 | 盘点取消 |

### B.5 对账差异处理状态（`m10_stock_reconcile.handle_status`，4 态）

| 取值 | 中文 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| `OPEN` | 待处理 | 新建差异，未分配处理人 |
| `HANDLING` | 处理中 | 已开始排查 |
| `RESOLVED` | 已修平 | 已处理且两侧一致 |
| `IGNORED` | 已确认可忽略 | 预占口径差异等不影响账实的项 |

### B.6 加工单状态（`m10_lab_job.status`，8 态）

| 取值 | 中文 | 说明 | 是否在制 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `PENDING` | 待派工 | 已付款建单，尚未派工 | 是 |
| `ASSIGNED` | 已派工 | 已指定加工师，未开工 | 是 |
| `PROCESSING` | 加工中 | 至少一道工序已开工 | 是 |
| `OUTSOURCED` | 外协在途 | 当前工序在外协供应商处 | 是 |
| `QC_PENDING` | 待质检 | 全部工序完成，待整单质检 | 是 |
| `QC_FAILED` | 质检不合格 | 卡点：必须判定返工或让步 | 是 |
| `COMPLETED` | 已完工 | 质检合格且已通知取镜 | 否 |
| `CANCELLED` | 已取消 | 订单退单导致加工取消 | 否 |

### B.7 工序执行状态（`m10_lab_job_step.status`，8 态）

| 取值 | 中文 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| `PENDING` | 待开始 | 前置工序未完成 |
| `READY` | 可开工 | 前置工序已完成 |
| `PROCESSING` | 加工中 | 已开工 |
| `OUTSOURCED` | 外协在途 | 已送外协 |
| `COMPLETED` | 已完成 | 已完工（不需质检或质检未做） |
| `QC_PASSED` | 质检通过 | 工序级质检通过 |
| `QC_FAILED` | 质检不通过 | 工序级质检不通过 |
| `SKIPPED` | 已跳过 | 可选工序被跳过，需原因 |

### B.8 外协单状态（`m10_lab_outsource.status`，7 态）

| 取值 | 中文 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| `DRAFT` | 拟外送 | 已生成未确认外送 |
| `SENT` | 已外送 | 已送出，等待回片 |
| `RECEIVED` | 已回片 | 实物已回，待验收 |
| `INSPECTED` | 验收通过 | 可继续后道工序 |
| `REJECTED` | 验收退回 | 不合格，需重新外送 |
| `CLOSED` | 已关闭 | 费用与工序都已结清 |
| `CANCELLED` | 已取消 | 外协取消 |

### B.9 质检处置决定（`m10_lab_qc.dispose_decision`，5 类）

| 取值 | 中文 | 后续动作 |
|---|---|---|
| `RELEASE` | 放行 | 加工单转 `COMPLETED` |
| `REWORK` | 返工 | 新建 `REWORK` 加工单 |
| `CONCESSION` | 让步接受 | 需店长审批，转 `COMPLETED` |
| `SCRAP` | 报废 | 重新下单制作，损失计入质量成本 |
| `RETURN_TO_SUPPLIER` | 退回供应商 | 外协件退回，换件或重做 |

## 附录 C：关键索引与查询

**C.1 P-18 可销售量查询（最高频，P95 ≤ 80ms）**

```sql
-- 一次查询最多 100 个 SKU 的可销售量（门店维度）
SELECT b.store_id,
       b.sku_key,
       b.product_code,
       b.product_name,
       SUM(b.qty_on_hand)                             AS qty_on_hand,
       SUM(b.qty_reserved)                            AS qty_reserved,
       SUM(b.qty_safety)                              AS qty_safety,
       SUM(b.qty_available)                           AS qty_available,
       SUM(b.qty_available) > 0                       AS is_available,
       MIN(b.lead_time_days)                          AS lead_time_days
FROM m10_stock_batch b
WHERE b.store_id = :store_id
  AND b.status = 'AVAILABLE'
  AND b.is_deleted = FALSE
  AND b.sku_key = ANY(:sku_keys)
GROUP BY b.store_id, b.sku_key, b.product_code, b.product_name;
-- 命中 idx_m10_stock_batch_sku（部分索引，只含 AVAILABLE 行）
```

**C.2 加工看板查询（P01，按优先级 + 承诺时刻排序）**

```sql
SELECT lab_job_no, order_no, customer_name, product_name, status,
       progress_percent, priority, is_urgent, is_overdue, promised_at,
       current_step_name, planned_finish_at
FROM m10_lab_job
WHERE store_id = :store_id
  AND is_deleted = FALSE
  AND status IN ('PENDING', 'ASSIGNED', 'PROCESSING', 'OUTSOURCED',
                 'QC_PENDING', 'QC_FAILED')
ORDER BY CASE WHEN is_urgent  THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,
         CASE WHEN is_overdue THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,
         priority ASC,
         promised_at ASC,
         created_at ASC
LIMIT 200;
-- 命中 idx_m10_lab_job_board（部分索引，在制单常年 60~120 行，索引体积 < 1 页）
```

**C.3 加工师实时负载（派工算法输入）**

```sql
SELECT a.operator_id,
       a.operator_name,
       COUNT(*)                                  AS pending_steps,
       COALESCE(SUM(s.standard_minutes), 0)      AS load_minutes
FROM m10_lab_assign a
JOIN m10_lab_job_step s ON s.id = a.step_id
WHERE a.status = 'ACTIVE'
  AND a.store_id = :store_id
GROUP BY a.operator_id, a.operator_name
ORDER BY load_minutes ASC;
-- 命中 idx_m10_lab_assign_operator（部分索引 WHERE status='ACTIVE'）
```

**C.4 TTL 过期预占扫描（每分钟）**

```sql
SELECT reserve_no, store_id, batch_id, sku_key, qty_reserved, source_no
FROM m10_stock_reserve
WHERE status = 'ACTIVE'
  AND expires_at <= CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
ORDER BY expires_at ASC
LIMIT 500;
-- 命中 idx_m10_stock_reserve_expire（部分索引 WHERE status='ACTIVE'）
```

**C.5 每日对账（M10 侧汇总）**

```sql
SELECT b.warehouse_code,
       b.sku_key,
       b.product_code,
       b.product_name,
       SUM(b.qty_on_hand)   AS m10_qty_on_hand,
       SUM(b.qty_reserved)  AS m10_qty_reserved,
       SUM(b.qty_on_hand * b.unit_cost) AS m10_amount
FROM m10_stock_batch b
WHERE b.store_id = :store_id
  AND b.is_deleted = FALSE
  AND b.status <> 'DEPLETED'
GROUP BY b.warehouse_code, b.sku_key, b.product_code, b.product_name
ORDER BY b.warehouse_code, b.sku_key;
-- 命中 idx_m10_stock_batch_warehouse
```

**C.6 加工工时统计（P09，月度）**

```sql
SELECT s.process_type,
       COUNT(*)                                          AS step_count,
       SUM(s.standard_minutes)                           AS standard_minutes,
       SUM(COALESCE(s.actual_minutes, 0))                AS actual_minutes,
       ROUND(AVG(COALESCE(s.actual_minutes, 0)), 1)      AS avg_actual,
       ROUND(SUM(COALESCE(s.actual_minutes, 0))::NUMERIC
             / NULLIF(SUM(s.standard_minutes), 0), 4)    AS efficiency_ratio
FROM m10_lab_job_step s
JOIN m10_lab_job j ON j.id = s.lab_job_id
WHERE j.store_id = :store_id
  AND s.status IN ('COMPLETED', 'QC_PASSED')
  AND s.finished_at >= :month_start
  AND s.finished_at <  :month_end
GROUP BY s.process_type
ORDER BY step_count DESC;
-- 命中 idx_m10_lab_job_step_type_stat
```

## 附录 D：配置参数与字典

### D.1 系统参数（M01-10 字典类别 `M10_LAB_STOCK_CONFIG`）

| 参数键 | 默认值 | 说明 | 影响 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `lab.assign.overdue_first` | `false` | 逾期单是否排在加急单之前 | 派工排序键 ① ② 顺序 |
| `lab.assign.buffer_ratio` | `0.30` | 排程工时缓冲系数 | `planned_finish_at` 计算 |
| `lab.assign.capacity_buffer` | `0.20` | 可派判断的产能缓冲 | 派工算法的可派条件 |
| `lab.operator.efficiency.expert` | `1.00` | 熟练加工师效率系数 | 产能估算 |
| `lab.operator.efficiency.mid` | `0.85` | 中级加工师效率系数 | 产能估算 |
| `lab.operator.efficiency.apprentice` | `0.65` | 学徒效率系数 | 产能估算 |
| `lab.qc.escalate_hours` | `24` | 质检不合格停留超过此小时数升级店长 | `job_m10_qc_escalate` |
| `lab.rework.max_times` | `2` | 允许返工次数上限，超过需审批 | 质检返工判定 |
| `lab.outsource.recalc_overdue_days` | `3` | 外协逾期多少天强制重算交期 | 外协规则二 |
| `lab.notify.retry_times` | `3` | 取镜通知重试次数 | Celery 任务 |
| `lab.notify.channels` | `SMS,WECHAT` | 取镜通知渠道顺序 | 通知任务 |
| `stock.reserve.ttl_minutes` | `1440` | 普通单预占 TTL（分钟） | 表 4.7 默认值 |
| `stock.reserve.ttl_urgent_minutes` | `4320` | 加急单预占 TTL（分钟） | 表 4.7 默认值 |
| `stock.reserve.ttl_custom_minutes` | `10080` | 定制单预占 TTL（分钟，7 天上限） | 表 4.7 默认值 |
| `stock.available.cache_ttl_sec` | `30` | 可销售量缓存 TTL（秒） | Redis `m10:stock:avail:*` |
| `stock.reconcile.cron` | `0 30 1 * * ?` | 每日对账任务执行时刻 | `job_m10_stock_reconcile` |
| `stock.reconcile.batch_size` | `500` | 对账分批大小 | 对账任务 |
| `stock.policy.sync_cron` | `0 0 3 * * ?` | 策略同步回写台账的时刻 | `job_m10_policy_sync` |

### D.2 质检项字典（`qc_items[].item_code`）

| 代码 | 名称 | 判定标准 | 默认是否必检 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `QC_POWER` | 度数核对 | 实测球镜/柱镜与处方偏差 ≤ ±0.12D | 是 |
| `QC_AXIS` | 轴位核对 | 实测轴位与处方偏差 ≤ ±3° | 柱镜 ≠ 0 时必检 |
| `QC_OPTIC_CENTER` | 光学中心偏差 | 光学中心与瞳距偏差 ≤ 2.5mm | 是 |
| `QC_SURFACE` | 镜片表面瑕疵 | 无划伤、无气泡、无杂质、膜层完整 | 是 |
| `QC_MOUNT` | 装配合格 | 无松动、无缝隙、开合顺畅、螺丝到位 | 是 |
| `QC_FRAME_SYM` | 镜架对称性 | 左右对称偏差 ≤ 1°，镜腿张角一致 | 是 |

### D.3 缺陷类型字典（`defect_type`）

| 代码 | 中文 | 典型成因 | 默认处置 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `POWER_ERROR` | 度数错 | 磨边机参数录入错误 | `REWORK` |
| `AXIS_ERROR` | 轴位错 | 散光片装框时旋转 | `REWORK` |
| `MOUNT_OFFSET` | 装配偏移 | 光学中心未对准瞳距 | `REWORK` |
| `SCRATCH` | 划伤 | 搬运或加工过程磕碰 | `REWORK` |
| `POWER_MISMATCH` | 光度不符 | 厂家来片参数与订单不符 | `RETURN_TO_SUPPLIER` |
| `FRAME_ASYMMETRY` | 镜架不对称 | 镜架来料不良或调校不到位 | `CONCESSION` 或 `REWORK` |

### D.4 外协缺陷类型（`m10_lab_outsource.defect_type`）

| 代码 | 中文 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| `SCRATCH` | 划伤 | 回片后发现镜片表面划痕 |
| `COATING_DEFECT` | 膜层不良 | 镀膜不均、脱膜、色差 |
| `POWER_ERROR` | 度数错 | 外协加工后度数偏差超差 |
| `OTHER` | 其他 | 需在验收说明中文字描述 |

## 附录 E：建表依赖顺序与 Alembic 迁移

### E.1 建表依赖顺序（4 层）

```
第 0 层（外部依赖，先于本子模块存在）
  M01-08  mdm_optical_product / md_optical_spec / md_optical_process / md_process_template
  M01-02  mdm_staff
  M01-04  mdm_supplier
  M01-13  mdm_org（视光门店）
  M15-01  m15_warehouse / m15_stock / m15_stock_txn
  M15-04  m15_issue / m15_secondary_stock
  M16-04  m16_optical_order
  M03     mpi_patient
  M10-05  m10_customer
  M10-06  m10_order / m10_order_item
  M10-02  m10_prescription

第 1 层（无本模块内依赖）
  m10_stock_policy          策略表，被批次表引用（冗余回写）

第 2 层（依赖第 1 层）
  m10_stock_batch           台账，可售量生成列依赖 qty_safety
  m10_lab_job               加工单主表，无本模块内前置依赖

第 3 层（依赖第 2 层）
  m10_stock_reserve         依赖 m10_stock_batch.id
  m10_stock_movement        依赖 m10_stock_batch.id
  m10_stock_check           依赖 m10_stock_batch（写流水时）
  m10_lab_job_step          依赖 m10_lab_job.id
  m10_lab_assign            依赖 m10_lab_job.id 与 m10_lab_job_step.id

第 4 层（依赖第 3 层）
  m10_lab_outsource         依赖 m10_lab_job_step.id
  m10_lab_qc                依赖 m10_lab_job.id 与（返工单）m10_lab_job.id
  m10_stock_reconcile       依赖 m10_stock_batch 与 m10_stock_reserve 的汇总
  m10_lab_stat              依赖以上全部（日终汇总）

说明：本子模块**不建物理外键**（与共享规范 2.6 一致），
      因此上表仅表示"逻辑与迁移顺序"，不产生 FK 约束依赖。
      分区表 m10_stock_movement 必须先建父表与至少一个分区，
      否则首次写入会因无匹配分区而失败。
```

### E.2 Alembic 迁移脚本

```python
"""Alembic 迁移：M10-07 视光库存与加工作业（12 张表）"""

from alembic import op
import sqlalchemy as sa
from sqlalchemy.dialects import postgresql

revision = "20261101_m10_07"
down_revision = "20261101_m10_06"


def upgrade() -> None:
    """按 E.1 依赖顺序建 12 张表。"""
    # ---------- 第 1 层 ----------
    op.create_table(
        "m10_stock_policy",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("store_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("store_code", sa.String(32), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("product_code", sa.String(32), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("sku_key", sa.String(96), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("product_kind", sa.String(16), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("policy_level", sa.String(16), nullable=False,
                  server_default="SKU"),
        sa.Column("qty_safety", sa.Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                  server_default="1"),
        sa.Column("qty_reorder_point", sa.Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                  server_default="2"),
        sa.Column("qty_max", sa.Numeric(12, 4), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("qty_min_display", sa.Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("reserve_ttl_minutes", sa.Integer(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="1440"),
        sa.Column("reserve_ttl_urgent_minutes", sa.Integer(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="4320"),
        sa.Column("reserve_ttl_custom_minutes", sa.Integer(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="10080"),
        sa.Column("allow_oversell", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("FALSE")),
        sa.Column("oversell_limit", sa.Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("reconcile_tolerance", sa.Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("check_cycle_days", sa.SmallInteger(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="30"),
        sa.Column("approval_loss_amount", sa.Numeric(14, 2), nullable=False,
                  server_default="500.00"),
        sa.Column("expiry_alert_days", sa.SmallInteger(), nullable=False,

        sa.Column("qty_reserved", sa.Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("qty_available", sa.Numeric(12, 4),
                  sa.Computed("GREATEST(qty_on_hand - qty_reserved - qty_safety, 0)",
                              persisted=True), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("unit_cost", sa.Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("amount", sa.Numeric(14, 2),
                  sa.Computed("ROUND(qty_on_hand * unit_cost, 2)", persisted=True),
                  nullable=False),

                  server_default="AVAILABLE"),

    # 流水表：分区父表 + 初始分区
    op.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE m10_stock_movement (
            id              BIGINT      GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY,
            movement_no     VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
            store_id        BIGINT      NOT NULL,
            batch_id        BIGINT      NOT NULL,
            batch_key       VARCHAR(96) NOT NULL,
            sku_key         VARCHAR(96) NOT NULL,
            product_code    VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
            product_name    VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
            txn_type        VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL,
            direction       VARCHAR(8)  NOT NULL,
            qty             NUMERIC(12,4) NOT NULL,
            before_qty      NUMERIC(12,4) NOT NULL,
            after_qty       NUMERIC(12,4) NOT NULL,
            unit_cost       NUMERIC(12,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
            total_cost      NUMERIC(14,2)
                            GENERATED ALWAYS AS (ROUND(qty * unit_cost, 2)) STORED,
            source_type     VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL,
            source_no       VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
            source_line_no  INTEGER,
            reserve_no      VARCHAR(32),
            idempotency_key VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
            occurred_at     TIMESTAMP   NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            operator_id     BIGINT,
            operator_name   VARCHAR(64),
            remark          VARCHAR(512),
            created_at      TIMESTAMP   NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            PRIMARY KEY (id, created_at)
        ) PARTITION BY RANGE (created_at)
    """)
    op.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE m10_stock_movement_p202611 PARTITION OF m10_stock_movement
            FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-11-01') TO ('2026-12-01')
    """)
    op.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE m10_stock_movement_p202612 PARTITION OF m10_stock_movement
            FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-12-01') TO ('2027-01-01')
    """)

                    ["lab_job_id", "qc_round", "qc_type"], unique=True,
                    postgresql_where=sa.text("is_deleted = FALSE"))
    op.create_index("idx_m10_lab_qc_fail", "m10_lab_qc",
                    ["defect_type", sa.text("qc_at DESC")],
                    postgresql_where=sa.text("qc_result = 'FAIL'"))

    op.create_table(
        "m10_stock_reconcile",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("reconcile_date", sa.Date(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("reconcile_batch_no", sa.String(32), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("store_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("warehouse_code", sa.String(32), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("sku_key", sa.String(96), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("batch_key", sa.String(96), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("product_code", sa.String(32), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("product_name", sa.String(200), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("reconcile_level", sa.String(16), nullable=False,
                  server_default="SKU"),
        sa.Column("m10_qty_on_hand", sa.Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("m10_qty_reserved", sa.Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("m15_qty_on_hand", sa.Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("m15_qty_locked", sa.Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("diff_qty", sa.Numeric(12, 4),
                  sa.Computed("m10_qty_on_hand - m15_qty_on_hand", persisted=True),
                  nullable=False),
        sa.Column("unit_cost", sa.Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("diff_amount", sa.Numeric(14, 2),
                  sa.Computed("ROUND((m10_qty_on_hand - m15_qty_on_hand) * unit_cost, 2)",
                              persisted=True), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("diff_type", sa.String(24), nullable=False,
                  server_default="MATCHED"),
        sa.Column("tolerance_qty", sa.Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("is_beyond_tolerance", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("FALSE")),
        sa.Column("root_cause", sa.String(24), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("handle_status", sa.String(16), nullable=False,
                  server_default="OPEN"),
        sa.Column("handle_type", sa.String(24), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("handle_by", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("handle_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("handle_remark", sa.String(512), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("created_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
        sa.Column("updated_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
        sa.Column("is_deleted", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("FALSE")),
    )
    op.create_index("uk_m10_stock_reconcile_key", "m10_stock_reconcile",
                    ["reconcile_date", "reconcile_batch_no", "warehouse_code",
                     "sku_key", "reconcile_level"], unique=True)
    op.create_index("idx_m10_stock_reconcile_open", "m10_stock_reconcile",
                    ["handle_status", sa.text("reconcile_date DESC")],
                    postgresql_where=sa.text("handle_status IN ('OPEN','HANDLING')"))

    op.create_table(
        "m10_lab_stat",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("stat_date", sa.Date(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("store_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("store_code", sa.String(32), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("stat_type", sa.String(16), nullable=False,
                  server_default="OPERATOR"),
        sa.Column("operator_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("operator_name", sa.String(64), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("product_kind", sa.String(16), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("job_created_count", sa.Integer(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("job_completed_count", sa.Integer(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("original_minutes", sa.Integer(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("rework_minutes", sa.Integer(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("capacity_minutes", sa.Integer(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("qc_count", sa.Integer(), nullable=False, server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("qc_pass_count", sa.Integer(), nullable=False, server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("pass_rate", sa.Numeric(6, 4),
                  sa.Computed("CASE WHEN qc_count > 0 THEN ROUND(qc_pass_count::NUMERIC "
                              "/ qc_count, 4) ELSE 0 END", persisted=True),
                  nullable=False),
        sa.Column("calc_version", sa.SmallInteger(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="1"),
        sa.Column("created_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
        sa.Column("updated_at", sa.DateTime(), nullable=False,
                  server_default=sa.text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")),
    )
    op.create_index("uk_m10_lab_stat_key", "m10_lab_stat",
                    ["stat_date", "store_id", "stat_type", "operator_id",
                     "product_kind"], unique=True)

    # 触发器：维护 updated_at（12 张表中的 11 张，m10_stock_movement 只增不改故不需要）
    op.execute("""
        CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION trg_m10_touch_updated_at() RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
        BEGIN
            NEW.updated_at := CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;
            RETURN NEW;
        END;
        $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql
    """)
    for tbl in ("m10_stock_batch", "m10_stock_reserve", "m10_stock_check",
                "m10_stock_reconcile", "m10_stock_policy", "m10_lab_job",
                "m10_lab_job_step", "m10_lab_assign", "m10_lab_outsource",
                "m10_lab_qc", "m10_lab_stat"):
        op.execute("""
            CREATE TRIGGER trg_{0}_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON {0}
                FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION trg_m10_touch_updated_at()
        """.format(tbl))


def downgrade() -> None:
    """按依赖逆序回滚。"""
    for tbl in ("m10_lab_stat", "m10_stock_reconcile", "m10_lab_qc",
                "m10_lab_outsource", "m10_stock_check", "m10_lab_assign",
                "m10_lab_job_step", "m10_stock_movement", "m10_stock_reserve",
                "m10_lab_job", "m10_stock_batch", "m10_stock_policy"):
        op.execute("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS {} CASCADE".format(tbl))
    op.execute("DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS trg_m10_touch_updated_at() CASCADE")
```

### E.3 上线前检查清单（10 项）

| # | 检查项 | 通过标准 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 12 张表全部建成 | `\dt m10_stock_*` 与 `\dt m10_lab_*` 各 6 张 |
| 2 | 生成列生效 | `qty_available` 在 `INSERT` 后自动算出，手工写入报错 |
| 3 | 部分唯一索引生效 | 同一 `(sku_key, source_no)` 插两条 ACTIVE 报唯一冲突 |
| 4 | 分区表可写 | 当前月分区存在，`INSERT` 无 "no partition found" |
| 5 | CHECK 约束生效 | 插入 `status='FOO'` 报 `ck_*_status` 违规 |
| 6 | 触发器生效 | `UPDATE` 任意行后 `updated_at` 变化 |
| 7 | `pg_trgm` 扩展已装 | `SELECT * FROM pg_extension WHERE extname='pg_trgm'` |
| 8 | 字典已初始化 | `m10_stock_policy` 至少有 4 门店 × 9 品类的 KIND 级默认策略 |
| 9 | M15 库房映射已配 | 4 个门店编码与 M15 `m15_warehouse` 二级库编码一一对应 |
| 10 | 首月分区已预建 | `m10_stock_movement_p{YYYYMM}` 存在下月分区 |

## 附录 F：本子模块专属约束与计算口径

### F.1 可销售量公式（唯一权威定义）

```
qty_available（本行可售量，数据库生成列）
    = GREATEST( qty_on_hand - qty_reserved - qty_safety, 0 )

某 SKU 在门店的可销售量（P-18 返回的口径）
    = Σ qty_available  (对同一 store_id + sku_key 的全部 AVAILABLE 批次求和)

其中：
  qty_on_hand   门店在架实物数量（非全院库存）
  qty_reserved  已预占数量（权威值来自 m10_stock_reserve 中 status='ACTIVE' 的汇总）
  qty_safety    安全库存（权威值来自 m10_stock_policy，冗余到批次行参与生成列）

【物理库存 vs 可销售量 —— 两个口径分别对应两个接口】

  物理库存（全院）  = M15 主库在库量 + M10 门店二级库在架量
                     → 查询接口：M15-01 GET /internal/v1/m15/stock/available
                     → 用途：盘点、资产报表、采购补货
                     → M10 不提供此口径（除 M15 外任何模块都不得"造"物理库存）

  可销售量（门店）  = 门店在架 − 已预占 − 安全库存
                     → 查询接口：M10-07 P-18 GET /internal/v1/m10/stock/available
                     → 用途：门市开单能否卖、选品工作台展示"还剩几副可卖"
                     → calc_formula 字段固定返回
                       "AV_ON_HAND_MINUS_RESERVED_MINUS_SAFETY" 供调用方核对语义

【为什么销售侧只能看可销售量，不能看物理库存】
  门店在架 5 副，其中 4 副已被今天的订单预占，安全库存 1 副。
    物理库存视角：5 副 → 收银员会认为"还能卖 5 副" → 超卖 4 副
    可销售量视角：5 − 4 − 1 = 0 → 收银员看到"缺货"，走定制或调拨
  这是 M10-07 存在的根本理由：把"物理上有"翻译成"承诺上还能卖"。
```

### F.2 四方责任表（库存唯一真相边界）

| 数据项 | M15 主库（权威） | M10 门店二级库 | M16-04 销售 | M10-06 订单 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **全院库存数量** | ✅ **唯一写方**（`m15_stock.qty_on_hand`） | 只读（对账时读取） | 不接触 | 不接触 |
| **主库锁定数量** | ✅ **唯一写方**（`m15_stock.qty_locked`） | 只读 | 不接触 | 不接触 |
| **门店在架数量** | 只读同步（`m15_secondary_stock` 由 M15-04 维护） | ✅ **唯一写方**（`m10_stock_batch.qty_on_hand`） | 不接触 | 不接触 |
| **门店预占数量** | — | ✅ **唯一写方**（`m10_stock_reserve`） | 调 P-19/P-20 申请变更 | 触发预占请求 |
| **可销售量** | — | ✅ **唯一计算方**（P-18） | 只读（开单时调 P-18） | 只读（判断能否交付） |
| **销售单与收款** | 只读引用 | 只读引用（`sales_order_no`） | ✅ **唯一写方** | 只读引用 |
| **订单业务档案** | 不接触 | 只读引用（`order_no`） | 只读引用 | ✅ **唯一写方** |
| **领用入库** | ✅ **唯一出库方**（`m15_issue`） | 依据 M15 回传明细建批次行 | 不接触 | 不接触 |
| **销售出库扣减** | 只读（不感知零售） | ✅ **唯一写方**（`OUT_SALE` 流水） | 不接触 | 触发取镜交付 |
| **盘点差异** | 接收 M10 回传并走 M15-05 调整 | 生成差异单、回传 M15 | 不接触 | 不接触 |
| **对账结论** | 提供 M15 侧快照 | ✅ **唯一计算方**（每日 01:30） | — | — |

**三条铁律：**

| # | 铁律 | 违反后果 | 技术保障 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **M10 绝不直接 UPDATE `m15_stock`** | 全院账实不符，财务无法追溯 | M10 服务账号对 `m15_*` 表只有 SELECT 权限 |
| 2 | **M15 绝不直接 UPDATE `m10_stock_batch`** | 门店预占被清空，超卖 | M15 服务账号对 `m10_*` 表只有 SELECT 权限 |
| 3 | **数量的每一次增减必须同时写流水** | 台账与流水不闭合，无法审计 | `ck_m10_stock_movement_balance` 强制 `after_qty = before_qty ± qty` |

**对账频率与责任：**

| 对账对象 | 频率 | 执行方 | 差异处理时限 |
|---|---|---|---|
| M10 门店在架 vs M15 二级库在库 | 每日 01:30 | M10-07 `job_m10_stock_reconcile` | 超容差 24 小时内归因，72 小时内修平 |
| M10 预占 vs M15 锁定 | 每日 01:30 | 同上 | 默认 `IGNORED`，只做趋势监控 |
| M10 台账 vs M10 流水（内部闭合） | 每日 02:00 | `job_m10_reserve_repair` | 自动重算回写 `qty_reserved` |
| M10 流水 vs M15 出库单 | 每月 1 日 03:00 | 月度对账任务 | 当月内修平 |

### F.3 预占两段式事务的错误码与并发处理

| 场景 | HTTP | 错误码 | 处理 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 库存充足、预占成功 | 200 | `0` | 返回 `all_reserved=true` |
| 可销售量不足（被别人抢先） | 200 | `"1007011"` | 批量预占整体回滚，`failures[].suggest` 给出定制建议 |
| 门店无此 SKU 批次行 | 200 | `"1007012"` | 建议调拨或转定制（`lead_time_days`） |
| 指定批次已被他人锁定 | 409 | `"1007013"` | 提示换批次重试，或改用 FEFO 自动选批 |
| 幂等键重复（重放） | 200 | `0` | 返回首次预占结果，不重复占用 |
| 预占记录不存在（释放时） | 200 | `"1007014"` | 视为已释放，返回 `released_count=0` |
| 预占已过期再释放 | 200 | `0` | 返回原状态 `EXPIRED`，不做二次变更 |
| 操作导致库存为负（并发扣减） | 409 | `"1007015"` | 乐观锁版本冲突，提示刷新重试 |
| 批次已过期/破损仍被预占 | 422 | `"1007016"` | 阻断，返回可用批次建议 |
| 预占 TTL 非法（超 7 天） | 400 | `40001` | 参数校验失败 |

### F.4 加工单两处卡点的数据库级保证

```sql
-- 卡点一：QC_FAILED 必须落缺陷类型与原因
--   若尝试把 status 置为 QC_FAILED 而不填 defect_type，写入直接失败
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_job
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_job_qc_failed
    CHECK (status <> 'QC_FAILED'
           OR (qc_status = 'FAILED' AND defect_type IS NOT NULL
               AND qc_fail_reason IS NOT NULL));

-- 卡点二：COMPLETED 必须已完工 + 质检合格 + 已通知
--   若尝试把 status 置为 COMPLETED 但 notify_status='NOT_SENT'，写入直接失败
ALTER TABLE m10_lab_job
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_lab_job_completed
    CHECK (status <> 'COMPLETED'
           OR (finished_at IS NOT NULL AND ready_at IS NOT NULL
               AND qc_status = 'PASSED'
               AND notify_status IN ('SENT', 'NOT_REQUIRED')));
```

**两处卡点的业务含义：**

| 卡点 | 若没有这条约束会怎样 | 约束带来的操作路径 |
|---|---|---|
| `QC_FAILED` | 加工师把单子标成"不合格"却不填原因，单子挂在 `QC_FAILED` 里无人认领，顾客等到过期 | 必须选缺陷类型 + 写描述 → 进入返工队列 → 有归属人跟进 |
| `COMPLETED` | 加工师做完直接点"完工"，取镜通知任务失败或漏发，顾客不知道可以取镜 | 必须质检合格 + 通知成功才能完工 → 若通知失败，`notify_status='FAILED'` 不满足约束 → 单子停在 `QC_PENDING`，被补偿任务盯住 |

### F.5 返工工时口径汇总

| 统计项 | 口径 | 数据来源 | 用途 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `original_minutes` | 首次加工（`rework_count=1`）工序实际工时合计 | `m10_lab_job_step.actual_minutes` | 加工师效率考核分母 |
| `rework_minutes` | 返工单（`rework_count≥2`）工序实际工时合计 | 同上，按 `job_type='REWORK'` 过滤 | 质量成本核算 |
| `outsource_minutes` | 外协工序的标准工时合计（本院未消耗） | `m10_lab_job_step.standard_minutes` 按 `is_outsourced=true` 过滤 | 外协占比分析 |
| `standard_minutes` | 全部工序标准工时合计 | `m10_lab_job.total_minutes` | 排程与产能估算 |
| `idle_minutes` | 班次时长 − 实际工时 | 计算得出 | 产能浪费分析 |
| `capacity_utilization` | (原始工时 + 返工工时) / 可用产能 | `m10_lab_stat` 生成列 | 饱和程度 |

### F.6 交期承诺（`promised_at`）的变更规则

| 触发场景 | 是否变更 `promised_at` | 变更方 | 顾客通知 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 建单时排程 | 写入初值 | M10-07 | 不通知（订单确认时一并告知） |
| 工序进展正常 | 不变更 | — | — |
| 加工师责任返工 | **不变更**（院内承担） | — | 不通知 |
| 外协首次逾期（< 3 天） | 不变更 | — | 不通知（内部风险标记） |
| 外协逾期 ≥ 3 天且影响交付 | 重算并**变更** | M10-07 发事件 → M10-06 改期 | 由 M10-06 通知 |
| 厂家来片延迟（`POWER_MISMATCH`） | 重算并**变更** | 同上 | 由 M10-06 通知 |
| 顾客主动改单 | 按新参数重算 | M10-06 改单流程 | 由 M10-06 通知 |

> **边界约定：** M10-07 只负责**计算**新的对客时刻并通过事件 `m10.lab.promised_changed` 告知；**修改订单上的取镜预约时间**由 M10-06 完成，**发通知给顾客**由 M10-06 调 M03-05 完成。M10-07 不直接改订单表、不发对客消息。
